景気の底堅さとインフレ圧力の根強さを確認。貿易
赤字はAI関連を含む設備投資需要などを背景に拡
大。非製造業PMIは拡大圏で推移する一方、仕入
価格指数の上昇は企業のコスト負担増を示唆

ユーロ圏総合PMIは節目の50超を維持。小売売上
高も小幅増加。ドイツの鉱工業生産は持ち直したが、
製造業受注は大型案件の反動で減少。景気は緩
やかな回復を維持するも、持続力には不透明感
欧
州
ASEAN経済は国ごとにばらつき。ベトナムは輸出や
鉱工業生産の好調を背景に高成⾧持続。フィリピン
では食料品やエネルギー価格高騰が消費の重石に。
タイでも原油高を背景にインフレ圧力が強まる
APAC
賃金改善が続く一方、個人消費の回復に弱さ。実
質消費支出は前年比減少が継続。消費者マインド

も小幅悪化し、物価高への警戒が根強い。景気動
向指数は低下したものの、基調判断は改善を維持
日
本
先週（10/5～10/12）発表の主な経済指標
米国では金融政策運営の変化と財政懸念、欧州では財政不
安が金利上昇圧力に。欧州では⾧期金利上昇による景気下
押し圧力が、ECBの追加利上げの必要性を低下させる可能性
金利・コスト上昇などの逆風下でも、設備投資意欲は旺盛。家
計の節約志向や企業間の格差は残るものの、価格転嫁・賃上
げも継続しており、日銀の想定するシナリオに沿った動き
注目点② 日銀さくらレポートは企業活動の底堅さを再確認注目点①米欧で金利上昇。異なる背景と金融政策への含意

米国：中間選挙を目前に住宅市場は軟調、アフォーダビリティへの不満が共和党への逆風に
 住宅ローン金利は足元で再び上昇
― 借入コストの負担増により、足元の住宅ローン申請件数は減少
 物価上昇が生活コストを押し上げるなか、金利上昇による住宅取得環境の悪化も家計の不満を助長する要因に
― トランプ大統領の生活費への政策対応は約7割が「期待より悪い」と評価しており、支持率は低下基調
―11月に控える中間選挙では、アフォーダビリティへの対応が引き続き主要な論点

米国：金融政策・財政の両面から米長期金利に上昇圧力
 住宅ローン金利の基準となる米国10年債利回りは9月以降水準を切り上げ、足元（10/6時点）では5.3％近傍に
― 構成要素をみると、追加利上げ観測の高まりを受け、実質予想短期金利が上昇。ウォーシュFRB議長の就任以降、フォ
ワードガイダンスの後退を背景に、経済指標への金利の感応度が高まっている可能性
― 加えて、足元ではタームプレミアムも拡大。中間選挙を控えた家計支援策への思惑などから、財政懸念が意識されてい

る模様。トランプ大統領は10/2、Medicare加入者2,000万人超への90ドルの一時金支給を発表
5

欧州：長期金利上昇を受け、一部ECB高官は追加利上げの必要性低下を指摘
6
 フランスでは国内政治・財政面の不安から長期金利が上昇。イタリア・ギリシャなど周辺の高債務国にも波及
― フランス国債の対独利回り差は急拡大し、一時欧州債務危機（2012年）以来の水準に。その後、大統領選の有力候補と
目されるルペン氏（国民連合）が財政赤字を縮小する意欲を示し、拡大はひとまず一服
―ECBによる介入（国債買い入れ等）の可能性については、複数の高官が検討段階にないとの見方を示す
 長期金利の上昇は需要を抑制。ECB高官からは追加利上げの必要性が低下するとの見方も
― エネルギー高によるインフレ圧力と金利上昇による景気下押し圧力が併存。ECBの政策判断は一段と難しくなる可能性
仏長期金利上昇の経済影響（左）と直近のECB高官発言（右）
（出所）LSEGより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

### Page 8

7
日本：金利・コスト上昇下でも企業活動は底堅く、設備投資意欲の強さを再確認
 10月のさくらレポートではAI関連需要の拡大とその裾野の広がりを背景に、企業活動の底堅さを確認
― 同一の企業コメントに登場するテーマの組み合わせを集計した共起分析では、「金利×設備投資」の組み合わせが増加。
金利上昇への意識が強まる一方、成長・省人化投資の旺盛なニーズは継続
 賃上げや資産効果が消費を下支えする一方、物価高を背景とした消費の二極化が継続
― 企業は価格転嫁を積極化するも、消費者の節約志向が根強く、商品競争力や顧客層によって転嫁余力に格差
⇒景気・物価は強弱入り混じるも、日銀の想定するシナリオに概ね沿った展開。12月利上げ見通しは不変
企業コメントの共起関係の変化
（注） 同一の箇条書きコメントに、双方のテーマのキーワードがある場合を1件として計上。
なお、テーマ間の因果関係を意味するものではない
（出所） 日本銀行「地域経済報告」より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
企業コメント概要
0 10 20 30

金利 × 設備投資
価格転嫁×企業収益
AI関連 × 設備投資
中東情勢 × 価格転嫁
節約志向 × 消費・販売
7月
10月
（件）
設備投資における金利負
担、企業収益における価
格転嫁の重要性を示唆
 AI関連需要は想定を上回るペースで拡大。半導
体製造装置に加え、工作機械や光ファイバー関連
製品など幅広い分野に波及
 金利・建設コストの上昇下でも、競争力維持に必
要な投資を継続。一方、採算悪化や調達コスト増
から投資を縮小・延期する動きも
設備投資・生産
 原材料・人件費の上昇を受け、企業の価格改定は
機動化。値上げ頻度を引き上げる企業がみられる
一方、消費者の価格感応度も高まる
 価格転嫁が進み賃上げ原資を確保できる企業が
ある一方、転嫁不足から利益を削って賃上げを続
ける企業も
価格転嫁・賃金
 株高による資産効果や賃上げを背景に、高額品・
高機能家電・旅行などの需要は堅調
 一方、物価高の長期化を受け、高年層を中心に生
活防衛意識が強まる。低価格品へのシフトや値上

げに伴う販売数量の減少も

世界経済：エネルギー・食料の供給ショックによる負担は、所得階層や国によって格差
 生活費高騰に伴うインフレの影響は所得階層により格差
― 食料・エネルギー支出の割合が高い低所得層は負担が大。IMFは低所得層に絞った所得移転が望ましい対応と指摘
 エネルギー・食料の供給ショックによる影響は国家間でも格差
― 先進国では、エネルギー供給ショックによる消費の下押し効果は低所得層で特に大きい
― 食料品への支出割合が高い新興国・低所得途上国では、食料供給ショックによる消費の下押し効果が相対的に大きい

 タイでは過去にも大規模洪水が発生。2011年には、日系自動車企業の部材調達が滞り、日本国内生産に影響した経験も
―2011年の洪水発生時には、工場の被災や物流停滞、人員不足が発生。タイの製造業稼働率は一時30％近く低下
― 当時浸水した工業団地では日系企業約450社が被災。タイ製部品の調達難により、日本でも完成車が一時減産に
 今次局面では、現状、主要拠点への直接被害は確認されておらず、一部工場の生産停止にとどまっている模様
―10月頭時点では各社、大規模な設備被害や減産は見込まず、日本の部材調達への影響も限定的となる見込み
― ただし、10月中旬にかけ更なる大雨が予想されており、操業停止の長期化や被害地域の拡大には依然警戒が必要

日本：個人消費は耐久財特需が剥落。今後は良好な所得環境を背景に緩やかに拡大
12
 8月の消費活動指数は、耐久財特需の剥落などにより、前年比＋0.9％と伸びが鈍化
― 耐久財消費は春先の自動車特需の反動から減少。食品などの非耐久財も、根強い節約志向を背景に弱含みが継続
― 底堅く推移してきたサービス消費も、台風・大雨や猛暑による外出控えの影響などから拡大ペースが鈍化
 今後は良好な所得環境が消費を下支え。個人消費は緩慢なペースながら、底堅く推移する見通し
― 実質所定内給与はプラス圏の伸びを維持しており、基調的な所得環境は良好。増加基調の購買力が消費を下支え
― ただし、インフレ率の再上昇で消費の回復ペースは緩慢。マインドの改善が一服したことも回復ペースを抑える要因に

（注） 旅行収支要因は、非居住者による国内消費（インバウンド消費）を控除し、居住者に
よる海外消費（アウトバウンド消費）を加えるための調整要因。アウトバウンド消費は
SNAの家計最終消費支出に含まれるが、GDPの計算上は輸入として控除される

日本：景気ウォッチャーは消費の緩やかな持ち直しを示唆も、家計・企業とも先行きには慎重
13
 家計動向（現状判断DI）は改善。9月の消費回復を示唆するも、回復ペースは緩慢
― 特にサービス関連の上昇が顕著。8月に増勢が一服したサービス消費は、大型連休や秋の行楽需要を受け持ち直し
― ただし、先行き判断DIは低下しており、個人消費全体でみれば緩やかな回復であることを示唆
 一方、企業動向は、現状・先行きとも慎重。景気回復の勢いは強くない
― 企業動向（現状判断DI）は製造業・非製造業ともに低下。AI関連など需要は堅調も、資材高などの供給制約が重石に

― 先行き判断についても、原材料高や金利上昇、中東情勢の不透明感などが重石となっている模様

日本も「インフレ・金利のある世界」へ。
本稿では下記3つの疑問を解説。
Q1. 足元までの金利上昇は「良い金利上昇」か「悪い金利
上昇」か？
Q2. 先行きの金利上昇の「質」を左右するポイントは何か？
Q3. 日本はどちらに向かうのか、何に注視していく必要がある
か？
問われるのは「良い金利上昇」か
「悪い金利上昇」のどちらに向か
うかで、成⾧戦略、技術革新、
財政規律、市場環境が論点と
なる。

確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決

通膨升溫壓抑美股表現，近期聚焦財報結果
2026 年 10 月 15 日
一、 市場動態
1. 科技股領跌，費半連 6 日收黑：美股主要指數 10 月 14 日全數下跌，道瓊工業指數下
跌 0.09%，收 51,621.48 點；S&P 500 指數下跌 1.11%，收 7,718.94 點，為 7 月 29
日以來最大單日跌幅；Nasdaq 綜合指數下跌 1.88%，收 26,692.87 點，為 7 月 23
日以來最大單日跌幅。費城半導體指數下跌 4.30%，收 11,684.38 點，連續第６個交
易日走低，為 2025 年 5 月以來最長連跌紀錄。

2. 類股非全面下挫，資金仍呈輪動現象：半導體及 AI 相關股票在 10/14 跌幅尤其明顯
，AMD 下跌 5.73%、Nvidia 下跌 3.16%、Broadcom 下跌 3.10%，AI 基礎建設相關
的 CoreWeave、Nebius、Oracle 及 Dell 跌幅更深，顯示市場對資本支出規模較大、
融資成本敏感度較高的 AI 相關企業風險偏好明顯下降。不過，S&P 500 的 11 大類
股中有 8 類下跌，資訊科技及通訊服務為主要拖累來源，但醫療保健、必需消費與能
源類股仍逆勢上漲。
3. 美國 10 年期公債殖利率盤中急升後回落：美國 10 年期公債殖利率 10/14 由 5.2%左
右快速升至約 5.33%，逼近近期高點；然而，隨後市場消化 Fed 官員較審慎的政策訊
號，殖利率自高檔回落，終場約收於 5.19%，較前一交易日 5.21%小幅下降約 2 個基
點。
二、 下跌原因分析
1. 美 9 月通膨高於預期 ，Fed 再升息風險重新升高 ：美國 10/14 公布 9 月 CPI 月增 0.6%

、年增率也由 8 月的 3.4%上揚至 3.7%；核心 CPI 月增 0.3%、年增率由 8 月的 2.4%
升至 2.6%。其中汽油價格單月大漲 9.1%，貢獻整體 CPI 月增幅超過一半；住宅成本
亦月增 0.4%，顯示物價壓力仍具黏性。此數據公布後，市場重新提高 Fed 進一步升
息的預期 ，CME FedWatch 所反映的 10 月升息 1 碼機率一度由前一日約 22%提高至
37%。雖然截至 10/15 台北時間早上 7 點左右該機率回降至 29.3%，這也提醒投資人
Fed 的升息路徑仍存在不確定性。
2. 高利率同時壓縮科技股估值與提高 AI 融資成本 ：科技及半導體公司未來獲利成長占評
價的比重較高，因此對折現率變化相對敏感。當長天期美債殖利率維持 5%以上，市
場願意支付的本益比自然受到限制，即使企業獲利仍維持成長，股價也可能因評價倍
數下降而出現修正。另一方面，本輪 AI 投資需要大量資料中心、伺服器、電力與網路
基礎設施，高利率亦直接增加企業融資成本，反映信用市場對高度依賴外部資金的 AI

基礎建設公司要求更高風險溢酬。

### Page 2

確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決
策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
3. AI 投資焦點由「支出規模」轉向「投資報酬率」:近期市場對 AI 的疑慮逐漸由「需求
是否成長」 ，轉向「龐大的資本支出能否轉化為足夠營收、獲利與自由現金流」 。
Broadcom 執行長陳福陽近期亦指出 ，AI 未來發展的重要限制可能逐漸由晶片或記憶
體供應轉向資本本身。我們認為，這並不代表AI 資本支出週期即將結束，而是市場已
由第一階段的 「資本支出愈大、成長想像愈高」 ，逐步進入更重視資本效率與投資報酬
率的第二階段。未來財務結構、現金流與AI 商業化能力，將成為不同企業評價分化的
重要因素。
4. 中國記憶體競爭疑慮加重半導體短線壓力：中國長鑫存儲（CXMT）10/10 傳出記憶

體技術取得進展 ， 引發市場對未來DRAM 供給增加及價格競爭的疑慮 ， 記憶體相關類
股率先承壓，並進一步影響整體半導體市場情緒。惟 DRAM 與 GPU、客製化 ASIC、
先進晶圓製程及先進封裝的產業競爭與需求結構並不相同。我們認為，儘管記憶體競
爭升溫值得關注，但目前尚不足以延伸為整體 AI 半導體需求同步轉弱。
5. 高檔籌碼加速獲利了結：AI 與半導體仍是 2026 年美股表現最強勢的投資主題之一，
部分科技股今年累計漲幅已相當可觀。當通膨、殖利率及AI 投資報酬疑慮同時升高，
先前漲幅較大、投資人部位較集中的族群自然成為優先調節對象。因此，本波費半快
速修正除了總經與基本面疑慮外，亦包含籌碼降溫及風險部位收縮因素，使短期價格
波動幅度可能高於企業基本面實際變化。
三、市場看法
1. 類股輪動明顯，尚非全面看空美股：科技與 AI 相關股票 10 月 14 日明顯承壓，但醫
療保健、必需消費與能源類股仍逆勢上漲，道瓊工業指數僅下跌 0.09%。此外，S&P
500 成分股下跌家數與上漲家數之比約為 1.1：1；相較於指數下跌 1.11%，市場廣度

並未同步大幅惡化。這反映資金主要由高評價且前期漲幅較大的科技與AI 族群，轉向
現金流較穩定的防禦型產業。現階段市場特徵較接近類股輪動與風格切換，而非無差
別的全面風險撤離，亦尚未看到美股企業獲利或經濟基本面全面反轉的明確訊號。
2. 美股科技股財報前風險降載，Q3 仍存在重新定價空間：近期科技股修正，費城半導
體指數連續６個交易日下跌，也可能部分反映第三季財報季正式展開前，投資人在股
價先前漲幅較大、評價偏高及利率環境不確定下，提前降低風險曝險。真正決定後續
市場方向的關鍵 ， 仍在於實際企業獲利能否支持目前的高成長預期 。根據 Bloomberg
季度獲利成長追蹤資料，今年 Q1 與 Q2 財報季期間的 EPS 成長率經驗顯示，隨實際
財報陸續公布，企業獲利成長曾明顯優於財報季開始前的市場預期。儘管這並不保證
Q3 將複製前兩季的上修幅度，但目前市場因財報不確定性所反映的風險折價仍存在
被實際財報重新驗證的可能。若大型科技及半導體公司後續營收、獲利與AI 需求指引
實際公布結果優於預期，近期風險溢價可望部分收斂；反之，若獲利成長無法跟上目

前評價，市場整理時間則可能進一步延長。

策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
3. 費半修正幅度大，但 AI 需求尚未出現全面反轉：ASML 在 10/14 所公布的最新第三
季財報反而顯示 AI 相關設備需求仍相當強勁，該公司表示 2027 與 2028 年規劃產能
可能仍無法完全滿足客戶需求，部分客戶亦已表達對 2029 及 2030 年設備需求的興
趣。儘管費城半導體指數近期市場情緒轉弱，但由 ASML 的財報表現來看，目前仍未
看到大型 AI 客戶全面取消晶片訂單、主要雲端服務商同步削減 AI 資本支出，或先進
製程需求快速降溫等結構性訊號。
4. 台積電營收顯示 AI 需求暢旺 ， 留意10/15 法說會內容：台積電 10/8 公布的第三季營

收達新台幣 1.49 兆元、年增約 50%，創歷史新高，並高於市場原先預估的 1.46 兆元
；9 月單月營收年增 54.6%。強勁的營收表現仍反映 AI 與先進製程需求具有支撐。目
前市場因此呈現「企業營收仍快速成長，但股票評價下降」的狀況。高殖利率環境下
，EPS 上升與本益比下降可以同時發生，後續股價方向將取決於企業獲利上修幅度是
否足以抵銷評價收縮。該公司將於 10/15 下午舉行第三季法人說明會。市場重點將集
中於第四季展望、AI 與 HPC 需求、先進製程、先進封裝及未來資本支出規劃。公司
對 AI 需求與產能投資的最新判斷，將成為判斷近期費半修正主要屬於評價調整，或產
業基本面是否開始出現變化的重要依據。
5. AI 與半導體進入基本面分化階段：我們認為，在高利率環境下，高度仰賴外部融資、
自由現金流尚未成熟的 AI 基礎建設業者 ， 受到的資金成本壓力明顯高於具穩定現金流
的大型科技與雲端服務商。同樣地，記憶體、GPU、ASIC、先進製程及先進封裝各有
不同供需結構。我們預期下一階段 AI 行情將更加重視訂單能見度、產品競爭力、毛利

率、自由現金流及 AI 營收實際兌現，產業內部強弱分化程度可能進一步提高。
6. Fed 路徑仍存不確定性，惟企業獲利可望提供股市支撐：美國 9 月 CPI 升溫使市場重
新評估 Fed 後續升息時點與幅度，美國利率政策仍存在不確定性，長天期美債殖利率
也可能維持高檔。不過，現階段企業獲利成長與AI 相關需求仍具韌性，若第三季財報

確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決
策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
延續前兩季優於預期的趨勢，企業 EPS 若能上修有望部分抵銷高利率對本益比的壓縮
效果。換言之，後續市場表現未必只由 Fed 政策主導，企業實際獲利能否持續兌現，
將是支撐科技與半導體股中期走勢的重要力量。
四、投資建議
1. 短線聚焦美股財報與 Fed 決策，科技股中期趨勢料不致有變：近期市場同時面臨財報
前的觀望態勢、美債殖利率偏高及 AI 資本支出疑慮等不確定因素，短線風險偏好可能
受到壓抑，後續宜關注 10 月美股財報對企業獲利與資本支出展望，以及 FOMC 會議
所釋出的政策訊號。中期而言，目前AI 相關企業營運與資本支出尚未出現全面轉弱跡

象，科技產業基本面仍具一定支撐，但在利率維持高檔及市場評價偏高的環境下，後
續宜更加重視企業獲利、現金流及資本支出的實際變化。
2. 中天期債券風險報酬仍較均衡 ：美國 9 月 CPI 升溫使短端殖利率持續受到 Fed 政策路
徑影響，而 20～30 年超長端仍面臨財政赤字、國債供給與期限溢價偏高等結構性壓
力。相較之下，目前殖利率已位於歷史相對高檔，若後續核心通膨或能源價格改善，
中段殖利率具有回落條件，同時其存續期間風險又低於超長端，因此現階段以中天期
美國公債及投資級債的風險報酬結構相對較佳。

聯準會9月
升息一碼
• 聯邦基準利率升至 3.75%~4.00%，經濟預測部分上調2026-2028年GDP成長率、失業率皆下
調，通膨降溫進程仍相對緩慢 ，點陣圖顯示今年還有升息一碼的機會 ，這次升息對股票而言 ，
聯準會沒有準備以大幅犧牲經濟與就業換取通膨下降 ，降低了硬著陸風險；對債券而言 ，短
端仍須消化更高的政策利率，但長端反而可能受惠於抗通膨可信度改善。
AI模型
開發放緩
• Anthropic、OpenAI及xAI相繼支持放緩最先進AI模型開發速度。不過，前沿模型開發速度放
緩，並不代表AI基礎建設需求同步轉弱。晶片、算力、資料中心及能源等AI基礎建設仍處於供
不應求格局 ，短期若因市場情緒或資金槓桿調整而出現回檔 ，較可能反映估值與持倉修正 ，

而非AI需求基本面反轉 。後續若前期累積的槓桿壓力逐步消化 ，且全球總體經濟及地緣政治
風險未進一步惡化，AI供應鏈仍具中長期配置價值。
台灣央行
• 利率決議重點： (1)台灣央行宣布基準利率維持不變 ，重貼現率、擔保放款融通利率和短期融
通利率分別維持2%、2.375%和4.25%。(2)放鬆房市信用管制 ，調整自然人第2戶購屋貸款最
高成數上限，由6成調升為7成。(3)上修今年經濟成長率及通膨率 ，GDP上修至11.48%、CPI
及核心CPI分別上修至2.03%及2.16。(4)央行預估明年AI基礎建設需求將持續貢獻台灣出口表
現，不過由於基期已墊高，預測明年台灣經濟成長率為5.82%。

貨幣決議與9月、7月聲明稿差異，不再強調能源衝擊 全員支持升息
• 聯準會9月升息一碼，聯邦基準利率在3.75%~4.00%。
聲明稿重點差異
紅色字體為9月的新增部分，括號內藍色字體為刪除的7月聲明措辭
FOMC以12票贊成升息一碼 批准發布以下聲明 ：委員會決定 ，將聯邦基金利率目標 區間上調至 3.75%至4%維持在
3.50%至3.75%，以支持聯準會的雙重使命。委員會延續其維持銀行體系充足準備金的政策。
儘管中東衝突在一定程度上帶來了高水準的不確定性 ，美國經濟活動持續以穩健步調擴張 。儘管地緣政治情勢 等因素
使經濟前景仍籠 罩高度不確定性，但國內支出保持韌性，生產力成長強勁，企業資本投資也相當強勁穩健。新增就業

人數大致跟上勞動力成長速度，失業率也變化不大，顯示就業市場目前仍大致維持穩定。
相較於委員會2%的目標，通貨膨脹率依然偏高 ，這在一定程度上反映了部分特定部門 (包括能源)因供給衝擊所導致的
價格上漲。今日的政策行動，將有助於讓通膨更及時地回到2%目標，委員會將實現價格穩定。

聯準會會後記者會問答重點整理
1
通膨「過高且持續太久」 當
前重心偏向物價穩定
通膨數據未改善，通膨過高且持續太久，聯準會當前的政策重心偏向雙重使命中的物價穩定 。透過政策
行動表明，聯準會對抑制通膨是認真的。
2
經濟、就業與地緣政治促成
一致升息
近幾週公布的數據證實美國經濟正在走強 ，基礎經濟成長率高於先前判斷；其次 ，勞動市場依然穩健；
第三，通膨持續高於目標，地緣政治局勢也改變經濟展望 。華許表示，這三項因素共同促成當天「堅定
且一致」的決定。
3
點狀圖暗示今年再升 1碼 但
不是政策承諾
最新經濟預測顯示，多數官員認為今年仍有進一步升息空間。12 名官員預期年底前再升息 1 碼，另有 4
人預期還會升息 2 碼，僅 2 人主張利率維持在目前水準。不過，點狀圖反映的是官員對適當利率路徑的

個別預測，並非正式的承諾。
4
拒絕提供前瞻指引 不預判下
次會議結果
面對下一次升息可能落在 10 月或 12 月的追問，華許表示「我不給出前瞻指引。」
他強調，先前公開表達的是對貨幣政策紀律的承諾 ，而不是對特定利率決策的承諾 。聯準會不會預判未
來任何會議結果，後續行動仍將依據最新經濟數據及展望作出決定。
5
升息非市場逼迫 迴避川普施
壓問題
面對此次升息是否由債市波動及市場壓力所推動的提問，華許予以否認。
他表示是根據就業走勢、經濟強度及整體情勢作出決定 ，市場價格可作為觀察依據，但不能決定聯準會
的政策。
資料來源：Fed，華爾街見聞，2026.9.17

市場反應
• 市場將華許記者會解讀為偏鷹，那斯達克指數、標普
500指數、道瓊工業指數皆下跌。
• 10 年 期 美 債 殖 利 率 在 利 率 決 議 公 布 後 一 度 降 至
4.9323%，隨後回升至接近 4.99%，在關鍵的5%關
口附近波動。
• 2年期殖利率則由盤中低點4.5975%升至4.7124%，
反映投資人重新提高對後續升息的預期。
• 黃金跌破4,260美元。
• 利率市場顯示 ，交易員加大 聯準會在2026 年底前進
一步升息的押注，12月升息機率升至約50%。
投資建議
• 股市：AI長期趨勢不變，但需留意利率對評價面的影
響，逢拉回就分批買進，相對看好美、台、韓相關供
應鏈
• 債市：預期美債利率 維持高位震盪格局 ，目前債市

配置仍以鎖利為主，且聚焦中短天期。

台灣利率連10凍 明年經濟展望佳
• 利率決議重點： (1)台灣央行宣布基準利率維持不變 ，重貼現率、擔保放款融通利率和短期融通利率分別維持 2%、2.375%
和4.25%。(2)放鬆房市信用管制，調整自然人第2戶購屋貸款最高成數上限，由6成調升為7成。(3)上修今年經濟成長率及通
膨率，GDP上修至11.48%(六月預估9.45 %)、CPI及核心CPI分別上修至2.03%及2.16%(六月預估1.91%及1.90%)。
• 觀點：全球AI資本支出維持上調的趨勢，台股供應鏈訂單直接受惠，央行預估明年AI基礎建設需求將持續貢獻台灣出口表現，
不過由於基期已墊高，預測明年台灣經濟成長率為5.82%。
• 台股短線展望：上週隨著聯準會利率決議公布後，台股量能回升，顯示短線消息面或已利空出盡。權值股台積電領軍台灣加

權指數站上月線，技術指標偏正向。投資佈局方面，由於地緣政治、通膨及貿易關稅風險仍存在不確定性，建議AI概念持股
核心布局行業龍頭企業，留意中小型成長股較易受到外部因素而股價大幅波動。
資料來源：中央銀行，2026.9.17。f代表央行預測數。

美國零售強勁回升擴大內需支撐，惟價格壓力與升息提高市場波動
• 零售全面回升，消費動能展現廣度：美國8月零售銷售月增1.2%，創3月以來最大增幅，優於市場預期，並扭轉7月修正
後月減0.5%；非實體店面銷售月增 2.6%、電子及家電增 1.6%，餐飲服務亦增 1.2%，且扣除汽車與汽油後仍增 1.2%，
控制組更增1.4%，顯示消費回升並非僅由汽車與燃料支出帶動，第三季內需仍具支撐。
• 名目消費偏強，通膨與升息增添需求壓力： 不過8月零售銷售年增6.0%屬名目增幅，部分反映物價上升因素；同期 CPI
年增3.4%，汽油價格月增3.9%，能源成本上升可能壓縮家庭實質購買力 ，加上Fed於9月決議升息1碼，後續利率政策
仍將視通膨與經濟數據調整，使市場焦點由消費回升轉向評估高利率與高物價對後續需求動能的影響。

• 後市觀察：短期零售回升仍有助支撐美國景氣，但若能源成本持續走高，加上升息後借貸成本上升，消費動能能否維持
將成為重要觀察點；投資配置可偏向美國投資級短中天期債券 ，兼顧收益與利率風險，股票則可聚焦獲利穩定、具定價
能力的大型優質企業，降低需求放緩與資金成本升高的影響。
近三月美國零售銷售月率變化(%)

顧 • AI安全監管升溫，短期壓抑市場情緒：Anthropic、OpenAI及xAI相繼支持放緩最先進AI模型開發速度，並引入獨立第三方
評估等安全機制，引發市場對AI資本支出降溫的疑慮。不過，前沿模型開發速度放緩，並不代表AI基礎建設需求同步轉弱。
Alphabet、微軟及亞馬遜近期AI相關積壓訂單持續攀升，合計達約1.64兆美元，其中微軟及Alphabet分別突破6,000億及
5,000億美元，搭配雲端業務持續成長，顯示企業AI需求仍具韌性，相關訂單有望逐步轉化為未來數年的營收與獲利。
• 槓桿壓力逐步消化，AI供應鏈仍具中長期配置價值：美國雲端巨頭擴大AI資本支出，壓抑短期自由現金流；相較之下，亞洲
科技企業自由現金流可望由目前約2,450億增至2028年的6,330億美元，支撐亞洲科技供應鏈中長期發展。短期若因市場情

緒或資金槓桿調整而出現回檔，較可能反映估值與持倉修正，而非AI需求基本面反轉。後續若前期累積的槓桿壓力逐步消化，
且全球總體經濟及地緣政治風險未進一步惡化，AI供應鏈仍具中長期配置價值。

油價在美國中期選舉前，或將維持在高檔
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.14
伊朗戰爭期間卡達油氣收入暴跌
卡達政府的石油和天然氣收入 美伊衝突
• 美伊衝突短期內仍難見明確解方，彭博預估雙方後續可能採取的行動，主要可分為以下四種情境：
(1)伊朗承受經濟封鎖：伊朗承受美國經濟制裁與施壓，避免進一步重大軍事升級。在美方護航行動支持下，商船仍可維持通行
荷姆茲海峽，國際原油供應未出現明顯中斷，油價預估在每桶80美元區間波動。
(2)伊朗將攻擊範圍限於海灣地區油輪與美軍：伊朗主要鎖定海灣地區能源運輸船隻及美軍目標，美方則採取有限度軍事回應，

衝突維持在一定範圍內，油價預估在每桶90美元區間波動。
(3)伊朗擴大攻擊範圍：攻擊目標不再局限於海灣地區船隻及美軍基地，海灣能源基礎設施以及紅海等重要航運咽喉，均可能成
為目標。局勢升級期間，油價可能維持在每桶100美元以上，待衝突緩和後回落至70至80美元區間，此情境發生的可能性最高。
(4)美伊雙方全面擴大開戰：伊朗進一步擴大攻擊目標，導致美國軍事資產及區域能源基礎設施遭受重大破壞，甚至不排除以色
列重新加入戰局，油價可能升至每桶120美元。

全球債市拋售勢頭不墜，AI融資競逐資金推升美債殖利率
• 長端殖利率走高，財政與通膨壓力仍是主因 ：近期美國長
天期公債殖利率持續走高，其中10年期美債殖利率更突破
5%，創2007年以來高點；除能源價格上升推升通膨疑慮
外，美國財政赤字、政府債務及持續性的發債需求 ，也提
高市場對長天期公債供給與期限溢酬的要求 ，反映長端利
率壓力仍未完全紓解。
• AI企業擴大融資，增加公司債供給與資金競逐： AI資料中
心、晶片與電力基礎建設的資本支出快速增加 ，帶動大型
科技企業提高債券融資規模；截至 6月，華爾街市場策略
研究機構Yardeni Research在追蹤外國私人投資人過去一
年買進美國公司債約3,900億美元，高於美國公債約3,290
億美元，顯示資金配置確有轉向公司債的現象 ，不過市場

研究亦指出，AI企業發債對美債殖利率的直接推升效果相
對有限，應可視為推動長天期利率上行的眾多因素之一。
• 後市觀察：若財政融資與AI基礎建設支出持續維持高檔 ，
長天期公債仍面臨較大的供給與資金配置壓力；但 AI投資
仍具中長期成長動能 ，配置上可關注資料中心 、半導體及
電力基礎建設供應鏈 ，固定收益則以短中天期美債及高品
質投資級公司債為主，降低長天期利率波動的影響。

新興市場本幣債券擁高票息提供防禦力
• 高票息提供防禦力：對債券投資人而言，票息就像安全墊。當市場利率上升、債券價格下跌時，持續收到的票息可以部分甚
至完全抵銷價格損失。新興市場本幣政府債券今年的表現，比市場原先預期更具韌性。目前新興市場本幣債券的持有收益約
為4.54%，已經足以對利率風險形成相當程度的保護 ，即使今年存續期間對多數市場造成負面影響 ，票息收益仍讓整體報酬
維持正數。這也是為什麼新興市場本幣債券在美伊衝突爆發後一度重挫超過 6%，但之後仍能逐步反彈 。哥倫比亞、巴西、
匈牙利及墨西哥的債券表現尤其突出。
• 市場已反映相當程度的升息風險：問題在於未來幾個月投資環境可能比上半年更加困難，能源價格上漲推升通膨，也迫使新
興市場央行維持較為鷹派的貨幣政策 。市場目前甚至預期 ，19個主要新興市場中 ，有18個市場的債券殖利率可能在年底前

上升。殖利率上升意味著債券價格承壓，因此投資人不能只看表面的高票息，必須同時評估未來利率變化可能造成資本損失。

資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.15

新興市場本幣債券目前最大風險在於利率與匯率
• 存續期間仍是今年最主要的拖累因素：一般而言，債券的存續期間越高，當殖利率上升時，債券價格下跌的幅度就可能越大。
今年19個符合基準指數條件的新興市場中 ，有14個市場的總報酬受到存續期間拖累 ，其中韓國、馬來西亞、印尼、泰國及
菲律賓受到的負面影響最為明顯。更值得注意的是，新興市場債券的利率表現與美國公債高度相關，因此，投資新興市場債
券，不能只觀察當地央行，也必須密切留意美國聯準會利率政策及美國公債殖利率走勢。
• 匯率可能成為下一個重要變數：新興市場本幣債券除了債券價格本身，還存在另一項重要風險：匯率。今年即期匯率變動反
而對新興市場本幣債券帶來正面貢獻 ，約增加57個基點。但如果能源價格持續上漲並形成滯脹環境 ，順周期貨幣可能面臨

更大的貶值壓力 。目前市場預期年底前新興市場貨幣整體仍偏弱 ，19種新興市場貨幣中 ，有11種預計兌美元貶值 。其中土
耳其、哥倫比亞及巴西貨幣的潛在跌幅相對較大。這代表即使債券本身提供不錯的票息，如果當地貨幣大幅貶值，換算成美
元或其他投資人本國貨幣後，仍可能侵蝕甚至吞噬債券收益。
• 小結與投資建議：目前新興市場本幣債券比較像是高票息換取較高波動的投資，而不是單純追求資本利得的交易。在殖利率
已處於相對高檔、但利率與匯率風險仍未完全解除的情況下，採取分散市場、控制部位、逢回布局的策略會比追高更為合適。

• 多重資產配置可有效風險管理：美伊衝突持續及聯準會利率政策，使今年金融市場震盪加劇，加上第四季美國期中選舉到來，
增加市場更多不確定性，不過從歷史經驗來看，地緣政治事件對股市的影響多屬短期現象，市場在初期情緒性下跌後，通常會
逐步回歸基本面，相較於預測事件發展，投資人更應透過多元資產配置分散風險，以提升投資組合的穩定性與長期抗震能力。
• 透過多重資產廣納收益來源：在利率維持相對高檔的環境下，投資人更應重視收益來源的多元化，從下圖可見，相較於過去幾
年，掩護性買權、非投資等級債，及投資等級債等資產的收益率均顯著提升，提供投資組合更具吸引力的現金流來源，其中掩
護性買權收益率已超過10%，非投資等級債，及投資等級債亦分別達7%及5%以上水準，透過結合不同收益型資產，不僅有助

提升整體收益潛力，更能降低對單一市場或利率變動的依賴，在市場波動加劇時提高投資組合的穩定度與韌性，兼顧收益與長
期成長目標。

2. 本資料提及之經濟走勢預測不必然代表基金之績效，基金投資風險請詳閱基金公開說明書。

前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 本資料所載內容如涉及商品資訊 ，商品相關資訊仍以經核可之條款及文宣 (DM)為主，本行網站已備有公開說明書可供查閱基金應負擔之費用及相關投資風險 。投
資人申購基金係持有基金受益憑證，而非本資料提及之投資資產或標的。
5. 本資料所載內容取自本公司相信可靠之資料來源，且為特定日期之判斷，本公司不保證其即時性、精確性及完整性，亦不提供或嘗試遊說客戶做為投資之判斷依據。

資料內容邇後若有變更，本公司將不做預告或主動更新。投資人於決策時應審慎衡量本身風險，如因信賴本資料內容而做出或改變投資決策，須自行承擔結果，本
公司及本公司所屬員工，均不負任何損害賠償及其他法律上之責任。
6. 本資料內容之著作權屬兆豐銀行所有，禁止就所載資料任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。
7. 本資料所載部分基金涉及非投資等級債，投資人投資以非投資等級債券為訴求之基金不宜占其投資組合過高之比重 。基金經金管會核准，惟不表示絕無風險。由於
非投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，故本基金可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支
付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損。部分基金不適合無法承擔相關風險之投資人。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良
管理人之注意義務外不負責本基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
8. 基金年化配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除息日前一日之淨值×一年配息次數×100%。「年化配息率」為估算值。當月配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除

息日前一日之淨值×100%。
9. 基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動。投資人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績
效亦不代表未來績效之保證。本資料如以基金過去績效進行模擬投資組合之報酬率時，僅為歷史資料模擬投資組合之結果，不代表本投資組合之實際報酬率及未來
績效保證，不同時間進行模擬操作，其結果亦可能不同。
10. 部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之相關費用。基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部份，可能導致原始投資金額減損。各基金由本
金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至各投信基金公司及總代理人網站查詢。

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
Implications to our coverage: If next-gen AI server racks do not adopt PTFE-based
CCL, we think the outcome would skew positive for EMC and TUC but slightly

negative for Shengyi. We expect the material choice to be confirmed by mid-2027 at
the latest. Additionally, with EMC expecting its substrate CCL capacity to more than
double by end-2028, Unimicron and other substrate makers gain an alternative
supply source, which we view as positive. Substrate CCL carries higher gross
margins than PCB CCL, so a higher contribution from this business should improve
EMC’s profitability as S572T ramps from 4Q26. On ABF substrate, lead times of at

least two years for key equipment such as EPM’s ABF laminators imply that new
greenfield ABF substrate capacity can only ramp from 2029 onwards. This aligns
with our view that ABF substrate undersupply will deepen in 2027-28. Substrate
sizes going to 230mm x 230mm by 2028, implying only 4 substrates per panel, is
also supportive of our positive view on demand. This would make it very difficult to
control yield, as one area of defect on the panel would imply 25% yield loss.

PTFE CCL: Adoption of glass cloth-free PTFE CCLs in next-gen AI server systems
was the topic investors asked about most at the booths. As we have written
previously (here and here), pure PTFE CCLs could deliver the best signal integrity
(SI), making them the preferred solution for midplane/switch PCBs for some
customers. However, their processability at PCB makers remains a challenge for
now, particularly for high-layer-count boards. In addition, multilayer boards (MLBs)

using PTFE require special high-temperature presses capable of reaching close to
400°C, as standard vacuum presses are insufficient. In response, Taiwanese CCL
suppliers are also exploring other materials that could deliver M10-and-beyond
performance, including hydrocarbon (HC)-based, HC+PTFE hybrid and
benzocyclobutene (BCB)-based CCLs. We expect the CCL material for the next-gen
AI server rack to be confirmed by mid-2027 at the latest.
Other key takeaways can be found on the following page.

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
PTFE offers the best SI but is harder to process; TW CCL makers are less bullish
on PTFE and are also exploring HC, HC+PTFE hybrid & BCB.
Thin E-glass cloth shortages and price hikes to last through 1H27, at least, as
looms stay scarce and lines shift to LDK/T-glass.
EPM’s ABF laminators (95%+ global share) are a key bottleneck to ABF substrate

expansion, with 2+ year lead times and no new greenfield capacity until 2029.
Glass core substrate is likely not commercially viable in the next 2-3 years, with
crack inspection after ABF lamination currently being problematic.
October 26, 2026 01:42 PM GMT

### Page 2

M
Idea
2
Substrate CCL: S572T, the most advanced substrate CCL on display at EMC’s booth,
enters mass production in 4Q26, with volumes expected to increase sequentially
throughout 2027. EMC expects its substrate CCL capacity to more than double from
current levels by end-2028 and said it will have multiple major ABF substrate
customers. It added that it has qualified additional low-CTE glass sources to ensure
sufficient supply.
Glass cloth shortage: Several CCL suppliers still cite thin E-glass cloth as the key

material bottleneck. They generally expect the shortage to persist through 1H27
with continued price hikes, and one supplier believes it could last through late 2027.
In their view, the tightness in electronic-grade glass cloth stems from insufficient
supply of Japanese-made looms, as well as from existing glass cloth suppliers either
prioritizing in-house demand or converting production lines to LDK1/2 and T-glass
cloth to meet strong AI demand. One glass cloth supplier noted it remains reluctant

to adopt non-Toyota looms given the higher defect rates and repair costs.
ABF laminator: We also visited the booth of Eternal Materials (1717.TW, Not
Covered), whose subsidiary Eternal Precision Mechanics (EPM, 7795.TW, Not
Covered) holds 95%+ global market share in vacuum laminators used to laminate
ABF onto substrate cores. EPM’s ABF laminators are now one of the key
bottlenecks in ABF substrate capacity expansion. Our checks with EPM suggest
equipment lead times have stretched to at least two years and EPM’s new

greenfield capacity will not come online until 2029; until then, EPM can only
debottleneck by optimizing existing capacity and leasing idle capacity from third
parties. For context, a new ABF substrate plant may require at least 10 sets of EPM’s
most advanced three-stage vacuum laminator and pre-track machine, depending on
factory layout.
Glass core substrate: Microcracks and the resulting package reliability issues are
currently the two key technical bottlenecks for glass core substrates. Existing

automated optical inspection (AOI) tools struggle to detect and identify microcracks
in the glass core, and laminating multiple layers of Ajinomoto Build-up Film (ABF)
onto it makes inspection impossible, according to Shinko. One equipment supplier
also flagged metallization of high-aspect-ratio through-glass vias (TGVs) as very
challenging. We understand this supplier is not involved in copper pillar mass
transfer, an alternative approach to TGV metallization.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-27

Status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: Faraday released a mixed set of results and guidance on 27 
October. 3Q26 core results missed consensus on an unfavourable product 
mix and higher opex, while 4Q26 guidance was broadly intact on delayed 
MCU business rebalancing. Regardless of the near-term hiccup, 
management expects its 3 product lines of mass-production (MP), IP and 
non-recurrent engineering (NRE) to fire on all cylinders into 2027 for a 
double-digit % YoY revenue growth. This concurs with our turnaround story 
for the counter, and we reiterate our Buy (1) rating and 12M TP of TWD220.

What’s the impact: Results mixed, guidance intact. Faraday’s 3Q26 net 
profit came in at TWD279m, 9% above our estimate and beating 
consensus by a meaningful 38%. Despite the beat, dynamics were mixed 
(table on p.2). Core operations missed despite a higher top line, which was 
spurred by MP on stronger-than-expected MCU sales from its 50%-owned 
Artery (6907 TT, not rated) for a lower gross margin (GM) due to an 
unfavourable revenue mix as MP carries a lower GM than the other 2 lines. 
Higher opex, as a result of salary hikes and employee bonuses, further 
pressured core operations with operating profit missing our/consensus 
estimates by 36%/11%, but non-operating gains from marked-to-market 
investments more than offset the miss. Guidance calls for a roughly flat 
4Q26 top line with a 45% GM, broadly in line with our expectation.

Firing on all cylinders across all 3 lines. In our previous note, we 
expected rebalancing in the MCU business due to demand pull-in on price 
hikes and supply chain uncertainties. It failed to happen in 3Q26 thanks to 
early pull-in of OSAT capacity support, thus pushing back the rebalancing 
to 4Q26 — a key reason why top line was guided to be flat despite the 
other 2 lines of IP and NRE growing QoQ. That said, management expects 
the MCU business to return to a growth track in 2027 after the near-term 
hiccup. Its delayed advanced packaging (AP) ramp on design complexity 
and supply constraint looks set to accelerate in 2027 on OSAT capacity 
improvement which, together with a milestone of 2nm/GAA design-win for a 
cloud ASIC, should add to upside for the NRE and MP business next year. 
All in, we lower our 2026-27E EPS by 9-13% to factor in the higher opex.

What we recommend: Despite our forecast revisions, we keep our 12M 
TP for Faraday unchanged at TWD220 as we maintain our 4-quarter 
forward PER target of 37x. We reaffirm our Buy (1) rating with key 
downside risks of silicon inflation and geopolitical overhangs leading to 
global inflation and prolonging a non-AI recovery.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 6-9% below the consensus 
forecasts likely on the street’s lower opex assumptions.

27 October 2026

Turnaround confirmed (II)

➢ 3Q26 results a mixed bag; 4Q26 guidance broadly intact 
➢ Firing on all cylinders into 2027 after near-term hiccups 
➢ Reiterating our Buy (1) call and 12M TP of TWD220

Company profile 
Founded in 1993, Faraday is a hybrid design service provider offering IP and ASIC design services to 
fabless, foundry and system houses. It has R&D and marketing locations in the US, Japan, China, India, 
and Vietnam.

### Page 5

5

Faraday Technology (3035 TT): 27 October 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 10 Feb 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
Faraday’s Board of Directors (BoD) consists of 10 members, including 4 independent directors,
representing 40% of the board—significantly above the regulatory minimum of 20%. Three of the
10 directors are female, reflecting progress toward gender diversity at the board level. The roles
of Chairman and President are separated, supporting stronger governance checks and
balances. However, most non independent directors concurrently serve as executive managers,
which may elevate concerns regarding board independence and management oversight from an
ESG governance perspective.
Capital management 1
Over the past 10 years, Faraday has kept its dividend payout ratio at above 70% most of the
time, in spite of an up or down cycle. As an IC design service company without capex for
manufacturing fabs, Faraday's high dividend payout is appropriate, in our view.
Related party &
transaction 1
UMC, which is Faraday's largest shareholder, is Faraday's major foundry for its turnkey
business. For IC design service companies, a close relationship with foundries is essential since
their design should build on a foundry's process node and their turnkey businesses also require
a foundry's capacity support. UMC's support offers Faraday a competitive advantage, in our
view. Faraday has developed many standard cell and I/O libraries for UMC, covering logic and
mixed-signal specialty processes across 0.5μ to 22nm.
S Supply chainmanagement 1
Faraday has set several evaluation standards for its existing and new suppliers and it requires all
suppliers to get ISO9001 and ISO14001 certifications for quality and environmental
management. In 2024, 100% of its wafer, packaging and testing providers had ISO9001 and
ISO14001 certifications. In addition, Faraday also has on-site audits (quality system and process
quality audit, CSR evaluation and environment management system) for its suppliers with a mid-
to-long term target of conducting annual onsite audits for 100% of its suppliers.
E Water & wastewatermanagement 1
As an IC design service company, Faraday outsources its manufacturing and thus has no own
production sites. As such, Faraday's water use is primarily for general operations, not for
production. Faraday continues to conduct several water reduction measures to manage water
usage.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
1
Faraday has established a green supply chain in accordance with the EU Directive on the
Restriction of the Use of Certain Hazardous Substances in Electrical and Electronic Equipment
(RoHS) and with the EU Chemicals Policy (REACH).
Faraday has a mid-to-long term target of carefully evaluating and selecting raw materials with
low hazard levels to comply with the Hazardous Substance-Free Product Standard. All of its
packaging materials are 100% lead-free and halogen-free.
E GHG emissions 1
With Faraday's energy saving measures, it reduced the GHG emissions intensity gradually. As
an IC design service company without onsite production, Faraday's GHG emissions are much
lower than other semiconductor manufacturing companies. Faraday's low power design also
helps reduce GHG emissions. In 2019, Faraday completed the development of its ultra-low
power consumption 22nm IP solution.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

In Taiwan, we prefer TUC over EMC for greater pricing upside in
the next 12 months, given its higher exposure to midrange/low-
end CCL. Both stocks are attractively valued: EMC at 11x CY28e
P/E and TUC at 9x.
EMC (2383.TW)
• EMC has introduced a new expansion plan for its total CCL capacity to reach
11.95mn sheets per month (SPM) in 2029 and 15.25mn in 2030 (+14% and

+28% y/y). See Exhibit 3  for EMC's 2026-2030 capacity plans.
• EMC's substrate CCL capacity currently sits at 300K SPM. The company
expects to add another 900K SPM in 2H27, reaching 1.2M SPM by end-2027.
Our supply chain checks suggest that a US hyperscaler  will be the first key
customer of EMC's ABF substrate CCLs for its  eighth-generation ASIC (see
our  TPCA takeaways).
• Management noted a growing trend of customers requesting long-term
agreements that span at least three years, on average. These L TAs now

secure volume rather than fixing prices; they do not require prepayments
from customers. Despite the flexibility in pricing and payment, customers
will still face punishment if they fail to pull their promised volumes.
• We estimate that 4Q revenue will grow ~19% q/q, to NT$72.4bn (+190% y/y),
supported by pricing adjustments and continued mix improvement, given
limited capacity increase in 4Q. We see GM expanding 220bps q/q, to 39.9%,
for EPS of NT$53.33 (+32% q/q).

TUC (6274.TWO)
• Management expects price hikes to continue into 4Q, with new pricing for its
M7/8 CCL taking effect from November onwards. For M6 and below, pricing
will remain driven by raw material cost pass-through amid supply tightness.
We expect this trend to persist into 2027, with HVLP4 copper foil, E glass,
and LDK2 glass cloth to be the most undersupplied.
• We believe recent market concerns about TUC's "share loss" for an ASIC
customer are overdone. Given the ongoing supply tightness in the high-end

CCL market, it is normal for CSPs to adopt multi-sourcing strategies to
diversify supply risks. Meanwhile, we believe TUC is actively engaging and
sampling high-end M8+ grade materials with other major CSP customer(s)
for next-gen AI projects. TUC's share in a key satellite customer's next-gen
satellite products will potentially expand in 2027 as well, with materials
upgraded to M7/8.
• We expect 4Q revenue to grow 15% q/q, to NT$20.6bn (+126% y/y), driven

by pricing adjustments and capacity addition at TUC's site in Thailand site
(+100K SPM in 4Q, +200K SPM in 3Q). We see GM expanding 50bps q/q, to
37.4%, for EPS of NT$15.82 (+20% q/q).

Price Target Discussion and Valuation Methodology
Our price target for EMC increases ~5%, to NT$8,800, driven by increases in our

2026-28 earnings estimates: Our new price target implies 20x CY28e P/E (unchanged).
Our price target is also our base case scenario value. It is derived from our residual income
(RI) model (similar to the rest of our tech hardware coverage).
To calculate the value of the stock, we use the company's beginning equity plus the
present value of all expected residual income of earnings in excess of cost of capital. The
RI is positive when ROAE is above the cost of capital and negative when it is below the

cost of capital. Our key assumptions (all unchanged) include a cost of equity of 9.3% (beta
of 1.0, equity risk premium of 8.7%, and risk-free rate of 1.0%), a medium-term growth rate
of 12%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
The 20x CY28 P/E implied by our price target is slightly above the past three years'
average +1SD of 18x, but much lower than the 32x recent P/E peak. We deem this
valuation fair because EMC is benefiting from an earnings growth cycle fueled by stronger

AI server CCL demand, EMC's own mix improvement, and continued pricing adjustments,
which should support margin expansion.
Our bull and bear case values also increase ~5%, to NT$13,650 and NT$4,200,
respectively.

TUC: Earnings Estimate Changes
We raise our  gross margin and operating margin assumptions for CY26-28 to factor in the
margin beat in 3Q26. However, we also revise our tax rate assumptions from 4Q26
onwards to reflect the higher-than-expected tax rate in 3Q26 (31.1% vs. our 25.0%
estimate). Our  EPS estimates consequently change +3% for CY26, -5% for CY27, and -6%
for CY28.

Price target discussion and valuation methodology
Our price target for TUC decreases  ~6%, to NT$2,550, primarily driven by the changes
in our earnings estimates for CY27-28: This implies 28x CY27e or 17x CY28e P/E. Our
price target is also our base case scenario value. It is derived from our residual income (RI)
model (similar to the rest of our tech hardware coverage).
To calculate the value of the stock, we use the company's beginning equity plus the

present value of all expected residual income of earnings in excess of cost of capital. The
RI is positive when ROAE is above the cost of capital and negative when it is below the
cost of capital. Our key assumptions (all unchanged) include a cost of equity of 9.3% (beta
of 1.0, equity risk premium of 8.7%, and risk-free rate of 1.0%), a medium-term growth rate
of 11%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
The 17x CY28e P/E implied by our price target is roughly in line with the past three years'

average +1SD of 17.8x. We deem this valuation fair because TUC is benefiting from an
earnings growth cycle fueled by stronger AI server/networking CCL demand, TUC's own
mix improvement, and continued pricing adjustments, which should support margin
expansion.
Our bull and bear case scenario values also decline ~6%, to NT$3,825 and NT$635,
respectively.

MS Rating
▪ TUC is one of the few high-end suppliers
of copper-clad laminates whose M7/M8+
CCLs enable AI servers and 800G/1.6T
switches.
▪ Continued demand growth from AI
infrastructure build-out and LEO satellites
should support continued mix improvement
and earnings growth.
▪ Tight supply-demand conditions in the
high-end CCL market further add to TUC's
pricing power.
▪ Risk-reward is attractive with the stock
trading at ~9x CY28e P/E vs the three-year

historical range of 6-27x.

NT$3,825.00
26x CY28e P/E
In this scenario, we assume high-end servers
and switches show signiﬁcantly stronger
growth in 2027-28, driven by faster-than-
expected new data center build-outs and a

smooth ramp of new generations of
AI/networking chips. We also assume CCL
demand from key satellite and power supply
customers will be stronger than expected. In
addition, we assume a new CSP ASIC
customer win for TUC, which will start
contributing to sales from 2027 onwards.
NT$2,550.00
17x CY28e P/E
TUC continues to grow materially, driven by
AI ASIC server, power supply and LEO
satellite demand growth, and material CCL
upgrades.
NT$635.00
4.3x CY28e P/E

In this scenario, we assume high-end servers
and switches see sluggish demand in 2027-
28, because of slower-than-expected new
data center build-outs and signiﬁcant delays
in new generations of AI/networking chips;
we also assume CCL demand from key
satellite customer and power supply
customer will become stagnant in the near
to medium term. In addition, we assume no
new CSP customer win and share loss in
AWS Trainium project for TUC.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

營收走勢圖

研究員/莊佳蓉 EVA8894122@uni-psg.com

研究部對緯穎(6669)維持買進建議，主係：(1) 3Q26營收季增56%，
大幅優於預期；(2) 4Q26在AWS的ASIC氣冷出貨帶動下，預期4Q營
收季增個位數 ；(3) 雖T3水冷機櫃短期遞延， 但預估27年逐月攀升，
公司仍為主要供應商。(4) AMD MI450 於1H27年放量，客戶與平台
更加多元；(5) 新增之Oracle VR200預期的訂單約2.5K，約在27年
貢獻營收。 綜上所述，研究部上修 2026/2027 年營收至1.46兆元
/3.07兆元(原估1.27兆元/2.38兆元)，EPS上修至143.16元/241.16
元，目前評價僅8~9倍，位於低檔，給予12.5倍本益比，目標價3010
元(2027EPS*12.5PER)。

重點摘要： 
1. 3Q26營收大幅優於預期 
緯穎9月營收為1,726億元，月增19.6%，年增100%，整體3Q26
營收達4,346億元 ，季增56.2%，年增63%，營收達成率達到
135%(研究部預估)/128%(市場共識)， 大幅優於預期 ， 主係Meta
通用型及AWS T3氣冷出貨旺盛所致。3Q26財報尚未公布，研究
部上修至毛利率8.9%(原估8.1%)， 季減0.4pts， 優於原先預期 ，
主係通用伺服器占比較高， 產品組合較佳 ，對毛利率形成支撐，
預估3Q EPS約45.56元。

2. 4Q營運動能可望延續 
展望4Q26，雖水冷機櫃須遞延至年底至1H27才會逐漸放量 ，但
仍預期T3氣冷機櫃及通用持續出貨，預估4Q26營收約4,666億
元，季增7.4%，年增59.5%。毛利率8.2% (原估7.3%)，季減
0.7pts， 主係高單價ASIC水冷機櫃占比尚低， 預估EPS約45.18
元。整體而言，2H26在ASIC氣冷及通用伺服器帶動下，營運明
顯優於上半年

3. T3短期雜音不改長期趨勢，2027年水冷逐步放量 
根據供應鏈調查：(1) T3水冷機櫃初期產線磨合不順，客戶因
此下修今年出貨規劃。不過，2027年水冷機櫃出貨規劃仍逐季
攀升，在晶片端的水冷比重也提升 至50~70%，單價為氣冷3-4
倍以上，成為2027年營收成長的主要動能。在AWS下一代T4產
品，公司持續參與機櫃設計，有望採用全水冷設計，預期28年
出貨，為中長期成長動能。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

(2)MI450機櫃部分，Meta專案集中在2027年出貨，單價約450
～500萬美元，訂單逐漸明確，預估約可達5～7K櫃。(3) 新增
Oracle VR200訂單，預期約2.5K櫃，約在27年開始貢獻營收。
公司在GPU伺服器的客戶版圖進一步擴大。

4. 目前評價位於低檔，維持買進建議 
綜上所述，考量各客戶訂單逐漸明確， 研究部上修2026/2027
年營收至1.46兆元/3.07兆元(原估1.27兆元/2.38兆元)，2027
年毛利率雖然因高單價水冷機櫃及GPU機櫃比重提升，下滑至
6.5%(原估7.1%)， 但營收規模大幅擴張 ， 獲利仍可望顯著成長 。
股本部分，緯穎配發股票股利，除權基準日為9/8，配股率達
1.9828倍，股本由18.69億元增至56.02億元，EPS因此依比例
稀釋，故2026/2027年稀釋後EPS上修至143.16元/241.16元。
目前評價僅8~9倍，位於低檔，給予12倍本益比，目標價3010
元(2027EPS*12.5PER)。

附錄 一 ： 評 等 之 標 準 
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다삼성전자(005930) 분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다 - 기업.pdf

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
26년 3분기 환율 부진에도 영업이익 100조는 넘긴다 
삼성전자 26년 3분기 실적은 이전 전망치를 하회할 전망이다. 매출액은 195.2조원으
로 이전 분기 대비 13.8% 증가하고, 영업이익은 101.7조원으로 13.6% 증가할 것으
로 예상한다. 영업이익률은 52.1%로 전 분기 대비 0.1%p 하락할 전망이다. 원/달러 
환율 영향이 주요 변수로 작용한 것으로 추정한다. 2분기에 집행되지 않았던 성과급 
일부가 3분기에 반영된 영향도 있을 것으로 예상한다. DS 부문은 매출액 149.9조원, 
영업이익은 101.8조원으로 추정하고, 디스플레이 매출액 8.8조원, 영업이익 0.9조
원, MX/네트워크는 매출액 34.3조원, 영업적자 1.1조원으로 예상한다. VD/가전도 
영업적자 규모가 커질 것으로 예상한다.  
주주환원은 주가 상승을 견인할 것 
8월 21일 이후 주주환원에 대한 관심이 커졌다. 자사주 규모는 크지 않겠지만 배당
을 통한 주주환원 규모는 사상 최대 규모일 것으로 예상한다. 글로벌 대표 IT업체와 
비교해도 손색없는 수준이다. 메모리 업체의 주주환원 규모가 100조원 넘게 집행된 
적이 없어서 주가에 어떠한 영향을 미칠 지가 관전 포인트인데 긍정적 역할을 할 것
으로 기대한다. 자사주를 통한 EPS 개선은 아니지만, 안정적이고 지속적인 규모의 
주주환원은 주가를 한층 더 높은 수준에서 평가 받는 계기가 될 것으로 판단한다.  
현저한 저평가 구간, 이전 전망 유지 
삼성전자에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 메모리 공급 부족 상황이 장기화될 
것으로 전망하고, 이로 인해 수익성은 현재 수준에서 유지되고, 이익 규모는 안정적
으로 성장할 것으로 기대하고, 현저히 저평가 국면에 있다고 판단하기 때문이다. 목
표주가는 460,000원을 유지한다.

### Page 4

삼성전자 (005930)

4 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
반도체 Check Point 
1. 차별화된 주주환원 전략. 배경은 안정적 이익 규모 
2026년에는 대대적인 규모의 주주환원이 예상된다. 100 조원 내외 수준으로 
예상하는데 상당 부분은 배당으로 집행될 것으로 추정한다. 배당성향이 25%가 유지될 
것으로 예상하기 때문이다. 자사주 매입 여력은 20조원 내외 수준일 것으로 예상한다. 
주주환원은 주가 밸류 상승에 기여할 것으로 기대한다. 일회성이 아니고 장기적으로 
높은 수준의 주주환원이 가능하다는 것은 싸이클 변동에 따른 이익의 변동성이 
낮다는 의미이기도 하다. 
2. AI 모델 개발 속도 조절. 그래도 메모리 수요를 줄일 수는 없다 
Anthropic CEO는 현재 AI 개발 속도가 지나치게 빨라서 인간의 능력을 뛰어 넘는 
임계점에 이를 가능성이 높아져서 개발 속도를 늦출 필요가 있다고 언급했다. 이를 
Meta, Google, SpaceX 수장들이 공감했다. 우선 이들의 공감으로 AI 모델의 개발 
속도가 둔화될 것으로 보고 있지 않다. 또한, 이를 가정하더라도 향후 AI 모델은 
Agent AI로 진화할 것으로 보고 있다. 이에 따른 메모리에 대한 필요성은 급격히 
증가할 것으로 기대한다. 
3. 자신감 넘치는 HBM  
HBM4가 2026년 하반기부터 주력 제품이 될 것으로 전망한다. 26 년 3분기에는 
비중이 40% 내외, 4 분기에는 60%를 상회할 것으로 예상한다. 연초 기대 대비로는 
낮은 수준이다. 삼성전자의 점유율만 보면 연초 대비 높은 수준의 점유율이 예상된다.  
HBM4 물량이 예상보다 늦게 본격화되면서 2026년 HBM 시장 규모는 이전 전망 
대비 축소될 전망이다. HBM4도 늦은 출시로 연내 생산 물량은 이전 전망치인 130억 
Gb을 하회할 것으로 예상한다.  
2027년 HBM4 가격이 최근에 확정이 되었고, 이를 통해서 DRAM 수준의 수익성 
확보가 가능할 것으로 기대한다. 수익성 발목을 잡았던 HBM은 2027년 물량부터는 
영업이익 개선의 변수가 될 것으로 전망한다.

장기적 주주환원은 
안정적 이익이 뒷받침 
Agent AI는 메모리 
수요를 증가시킨다 
HBM4 안정적 진입한 
것으로 판단

동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

### Page 1

한화에어로스페이스 (012450/KS) 
일시적 부진, 4Q부터 성장 가도에 재진입 
2026-10-30

조선/방산. 한승한, RA. 고서영 / shane.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-9992

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 1,300,000 원(하향) 
현재주가: 760,000 원 
상승여력: 71.1%

Signal: 3Q26, 수출 물량 저조 및 국내 낮은 마진 물량 인식으로 어닝 쇼크 
Key: 4Q부터 내년까지 수출 물량 빠르게 회복하며 실적 성장 이어갈 전망 
Step: 연내 새로운 중장기 성장동력이 될 중동 수주계약 기대. 매수 기회

3Q26 Review: 수출 물량 납품 저조로 어닝 쇼크  
3Q26 연결 매출액 6 조 943 억원(-6.0% YoY, -34.4% QoQ), 영업이익 4,520억원
(-46.2% YoY, -66.9% QoQ)으로, 시장예상치(1조 1,615억원)를 대폭 하회하는 어닝 쇼
크 기록했다. ①폴란드 중심의 수출 물량의 매출 인식 시점이 4 분기로 이연되며, 수출 물량 
비중이 감소한 점이 컸다. ②내수 물량 또한 감소했으며, 특히 국내 물량 중에서도 상대적
으로 마진율 낮은 유무인 무기체계 초도 물량이 이번 분기에 집중됐다. ③내수&수출 전반
적인 지상방산 매출 비중 감소에 따른 고정비 부담이 컸으며, ④일회성 요인으로 대전 사업
장 사고에 따른 복구 비용 약 300 억원과 충당금 및 기타 비용 총 160 억원, 그리고 환율 
하락으로 영업이익의 약 10% 수준의 감소 효과 발생했다. 다만 4Q26 부터 이번 분기 이
연된 폴란드 중심의 수출 물량 인식이 반영되면서 큰 폭의 탑라인 및 이익 성장 시현할 전
망이며, 대전 사업장 또한 내년 상반기 가동 재개 예상됨에 따라 지상방산 부문은 전년 대비 
성장 폭 두드러지며 사측 가이던스 15% 매출 성장 충분히 달성 가능할 전망이다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 130 만원으로 하향 
한화에어로스페이스에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’를 유지하고, 목표주가 기존 160 만원에서 130
만원으로 하향한다. 최근 Peer 그룹의 주가 하락에 따라 멀티플 하향 조정했기 때문이다. 
폴란드 천무 유도탄 JV에 이어 현지화가 전제된 K9 3차 계약을 추진 중이며, 국내 장약 생
산능력 증설을 통한 유럽향 수요 대응과 미국 내 생산거점 구축까지 진행 중에 있다. 또한 
미국 차륜형 자주포 사업 시제 선정에 이은 궤도형 사업 수주까지 기대할 수 있다. 중동 국
가들의 방공무기체계에 대한 계약 논의는 매우 가시적인 상황이며, 사우디 MNG 사업 수주 
또한 시점의 문제일 뿐이다. 기존 유럽에 더해 중동은 현지화 전략을 기반으로 동사의 새로
운 중장기 성장동력이 될 지역이며, 올해 4 분기부터 해당 수주 모멘텀과 함께 큰 폭의 실적 
성장이 맞물리며 리레이팅 가능할 전망이다. 선제적 매수 기회라는 판단이다.

Document 237: Advanced economies _ Resilience to shock after shock (2026-09-24)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Advanced economies _ Resilience to shock after shock (2026-09-24).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-09-24
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

3
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoPerspectives / 3rd Quarter 2026 / September 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
History tends to repeat itself in advanced economies. Once again, growth ultimately fell short of expectations by only a small
margin in the first half of 2026, despite the conflict in Iran. As early as 2025, the impact of tariffs was less severe than feared.
This is a sign that structural factors (notably AI, defence and electrification) are underpinning growth in these countries

and are expected to continue to do so in 2027. However, there are significant risks to the economic outlook, primarily of a
geopolitical nature, with a significant upside risk to inflation and interest rates. The other instance of history repeating itself
concerns a period that those under 20 cannot have experienced: the period leading up to the 2008 crisis, echoes of which can
be seen in terms of growth, inflation, the direction of monetary policy, and long-term interest rates.

Advanced economies are experiencing trends similar to those they
faced nearly 20 years ago:
- Growth remains resilient in 2025-26 despite shocks, as it did in 2006-
07, in the major advanced economies.
- Inflation is above central banks’ targets: as in 2006-07, this calls for a
tightening of monetary policy.
- In Europe and the United States, long-term interest rates have re -
turned to their 2006-07 levels (1996 in Japan): a challenging environ-

ment for public finances in a context where public debt ratios are signi-
ficantly higher than those seen 20 years ago.
THE GLOBAL ECONOMY IN THE MIDST OF A GROWTH CYCLE
We are already well into 2026, and the picture so far is clear: glo -
bal growth is proving resilient. Following a US tariff shock last year,
growth is once again proving, for the time being, to be significantly less
affected by the conflict in Iran than had been anticipated when it first

broke out. Initially, however, there were fears that this new shock would
hit growth even harder. As with the tariff hike in 2025, the impact was
measurable, particularly in terms of consumer confidence. But in both
2025 and 2026, growth has also shown a degree of resilience.
The figures for the second quarter bear this out, despite the sharp rise
in oil prices, the recessionary impact of which is not yet apparent.
According to our forecasts, growth was expected to reach or exceed 1%

in 2026 in the four major advanced economies (the Eurozone, Japan,
the United Kingdom and the United States, where it exceeds 2%) for
the second consecutive year. This had not happened (excluding the
post-COVID catch-up period) since 2006-07. Growth figures are more
moderate (particularly for demographic reasons), but the private sec -
tor is not (at present) showing the same excesses (at that time, the
credit boom had preceded the worst crisis since the 1930s); although

debt levels have recently risen in the US tech sector.
The driving force behind the current growth cycle is clear: artificial
intelligence (AI). It is fuelling global trade and business investment
in most countries. This is clearly the case in the United States, where
business investment is growing strongly as investment plans in the
US tech sector are implemented.
However, the world’s major economies are currently engaged in a
broader push to expand their capital goods investment. Alongside the

ongoing development of services—particularly digital services—we
have seen a rebound in industrial production in most countries since
early 2025. The leading sectors are primarily technology equipment,
machinery, aeronautics, defence and their essential inputs (energy and
metals). This trend is evident everywhere, including in Europe, and
is becoming entrenched over the long term, with an improvement in
business climate indicators, particularly new export orders. Growth is

therefore expected to strengthen further in 2027, particularly in the
Eurozone, where German investment plans are set to gather momen -
tum and whose spillover effects will have a greater impact on the eco-
nomy.
However, not all sectors are experiencing the same momentum. This
is particularly true for the sectors that depend on household consump-
tion, which has shown mixed trends. Our observation that consumer
spending is more buoyant in the United States than in Europe was

confirmed in 2026, and the outlook does not point to a change in this
ranking.
EDITORIAL
RESILIENCE TO SHOCK AFTER SHOCK

### Page 2

4
EcoPerspectives / 3rd Quarter 2026 / September 2026 economic-research.bnpparibas.com
The bank
for a changing
world
While European and US households are experiencing more or less the
same energy price shock, inflation remains higher in the United States.
But this has not prevented consumer spending from keeping pace, pri-
marily because it is driven by the wealthiest households, who are less
sensitive to inflation than to the wealth effect. Furthermore, while the

savings rate remains high in the Eurozone, US households have been
drawing down their savings. This is also true of British households,
which explains why the UK has seen slightly stronger growth since
the beginning of the year. In Japan, government spending has partially
offset energy inflation (while other countries are doing so to a much
lesser extent than in 2022), which has supported consumption growth.
However, these buffers—reduced savings or fiscal support—have their

limits when shocks persist. The two major current conflicts (Iran and
Ukraine) are far from being resolved, and their impact is continuing.
This persistent geopolitical risk and its implications for inflation repre-
sent a significant headwind to the growth outlook for 2027.
HIGHER INFLATION CALLS FOR MONETARY TIGHTENING
Inflation is exceeding the target set by three of the four central banks
examined in this publication, which is another similarity (albeit with

different underlying causes) with the 2006-07 period. Japan is the ex -
ception, as government support measures for households are tempo -
rarily holding back inflation.
Apart from the Bank of Japan, which is engaged in a long-term norma-
lisation process, no central bank was expected to raise its key rates
in 2026. But they are now being prompted to do so for reasons that
vary from one region to another. The first is the conflict in Iran and
the resulting rise in oil prices, which triggered a rebound in energy

inflation from the second quarter onwards. However, since the conflict
was not resolved by the memorandum of understanding signed in June,
and given that the subsequent drop in oil prices proved short-lived,
inflation is expected to rise again. The rise in gas prices is expected to
exacerbate the impact, particularly in Europe, where inventories are
being replenished ahead of winter. Electricity prices are expected to
follow suit in most countries, but at a more moderate pace than in

2022. Indeed, gas is now used less to generate electricity than it was
at that time (thanks to the expansion of renewable energy), and to an
even lesser extent in France.
There is a significant risk that energy inflation will spread further to
other components of inflation in the future, and inflation expectations
could eventually reflect this. This is why the European Central Bank
has already raised its key interest rate twice (in June and September)

and, according to our scenario, will likely do so again in December. For
the same reason, we expect the Bank of England to raise its policy rate
in November. Although the cap on energy prices was maintained for
a long time, it has now been raised. As a result, inflation is expected
to rise between now and the end of the year. The aim will then be to
prevent second-round effects.
Inflation can also be a sign of (too much) economic strength. In the

United States, it has exceeded the 2% target since 2021. The main
argument in favour of a new cycle of rate hikes (following the one in
2022-2023) lies in the current resilience of growth and the improving
labour market. The Federal Reserve (Fed) must balance two equally
important objectives: maximum employment and price stability. Howe-
ver, unlike in the 4 th quarter of 2025, when the decline in job crea -
tion justified monetary easing (by 75 basis points), the current sharp

rebound in job creation completely reverses that analysis. According
to our forecasts, the unemployment rate is expected to decrease in
the coming months, which pushes back the prospect of a decline in
core inflation. This situation persists due to both supply constraints
(over which a central bank has no control) and excess demand (which
the Fed can, however, mitigate). Consequently, the Fed raised its key
rate in September and is expected to implement two further rate hikes

between now and January 2027.
In Japan, finally, while headline inflation has been brought down by
government support measures, core inflation remains significant.
Wage pressures are mounting against a backdrop of labour shortages.
The Bank of Japan is therefore being forced to accelerate the pace of its
monetary normalisation.
In all these regions, inflation is currently expected to peak in Q4 2026
or Q1 2027. It is against this backdrop of temporary inflation that rate

hikes could also come to an end by that time (excluding Japan). Howe-
ver, this assumes that once the energy shock—the course of which
remains uncertain—subsides, inflation will fall. The effects of El Niño,
which are still difficult to gauge at present, could take over. Inflation
would then remain above the central banks’ target for a longer period.
Under this scenario, rate hikes would go further than is currently en -
visaged.
THE RISE IN LONG- TERM INTEREST RATES: A FACTOR THAT WILL WEIGH

ON PUBLIC FINANCES
Monetary policies have also to offset the inflationary effects of fiscal
policy, particularly in the United States (where the budget deficit is
high and shows no signs of shrinking) and in Japan (where the new
government is pursuing pro-growth policies). In Europe, fiscal policy
is playing a much smaller role than it did in 2022. While the stimulus
measures implemented at that time did help reduce the inflation rate
in the short term (by nearly 2.5 pp in France, for example), by keeping

demand at a high level, they delayed disinflation and increased the
scale of the monetary tightening required.
The outbreak of the conflict in Iran acted as a catalyst: it triggered a
rise in long-term rates (which had been trending downwards at the
start of the year) to levels close to those of 2006-07. This rise is wides-
pread, but its causes vary. Inflation expectations appear to play a more
significant role in the Eurozone, whereas the rise in real interest rates

seems to be the main factor in the United States and Japan.

The bank

world
Higher inflation and more resilient real growth than feared point in
the same direction: stronger than expected nominal growth that is li -
kely to be sustained. However, in the long term, high nominal growth
tends to drive up nominal interest rates (as it is a positive sign of more
profitable investment projects), in contrast to the problem of secular

stagnation raised in the 2010s (which combined low nominal growth
with low nominal interest rates).
The other issue is the high level of fiscal deficits (too high for a period
of growth), with various situations. In several countries, the public
debt-to-GDP ratio continues to rise. In the United States, deficit reduc-
tion does not appear to be a priority, to the extent that it is expected
to remain above 6% of GDP at the very least until the end of Donald

Trump’s term of office (with little hope that the mid-term elections will
significantly alter this trend). In the United Kingdom and France, fiscal
consolidation has begun, but it is being hampered by the simultaneous
increase in certain areas of expenditure (debt servicing, defence and
healthcare, due in particular to an ageing population), which is slowing
the pace of consolidation. In Germany, the implementation of invest -
ment plans is leading to a widening of the deficit. While this does not

pose a sustainability problem, it does result in an increase in annual
debt issuance. In Japan, the budget deficit is kept in check by interest
payments that remain low, as the rise in interest rates is recent and
the average maturity of the debt is high (10 years). However, expansio-
nary fiscal policy could pose further problems in terms of debt dyna -
mics if it were to continue through the end of the decade. In Italy and
Spain, fiscal consolidation has been sufficient to stabilise the public

debt-to-GDP ratio (and even to reduce it in Spain), but the high level of
Italy’s debt service it vulnerable to a rise in interest rates.
In all these countries, the fiscal arithmetic is simpler today than it
will be in 2030. Indeed, in most cases, only half of the debt was is -
sued after 2022 (that is, after the end of the zero-interest-rate period).
The debt service has so far only partially risen, except in the United
States, where most of the increase has already occurred due to a shor-

ter average debt maturity (6 years). This means that, for all countries
combined, the rate of GDP growth remains higher than the average
interest rate paid on public debt (or the effective interest rate).
By 2030, the effective rate will have risen and caught up with
GDP growth. As a result, in most cases, the debt service will have re -
turned to the level as a share of GDP seen in the early 2000s. In order to
stabilise public debt-to-GDP ratios, primary balances will need to have

improved. This will even require the generation of primary surpluses,
particularly in Italy (which is already achieving this), France and the
United Kingdom. This is a crucial shift that governments will need to
introduce as early as the 2027 budget; otherwise, they risk postponing
the stabilisation of their public debt-to-GDP ratio once again.
Article completed on 17 September 2026
Stéphane Colliac
stephane.colliac@bnpparibas.com

Document 243: AI最危险的交易或许不在科技股，而在美元

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI最危险的交易或许不在科技股，而在美元.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

德意志银⾏警告， AI 泡沫若破裂，冲击可能从美股蔓延⾄美元。外国投资者持有逾 20 万亿
美元美国股票，若股市、美元与美国实际利率同步下⾏，传统汇率对冲机制或将失效， AI
抛售潮可能演变为美元危机。
摘要：
当 AI 泡沫开始消退，最⼤的⻛险可能不是纳斯达克回调，⽽是美元崩塌。外国投资者持有逾 20
万亿美元的美国股票资产，⼀旦 AI 驱动的抛售引发股市与美元同步下跌，市场将⾯临远超技术性
修正的宏观冲击。
德意志银⾏警告， AI 抛售潮可能触发美国股市、货币对冲与美元之间的危险反馈循环。与以往科
技股回调不同，这⼀次美元的传统避险属性正受到根本质疑 —— 强美元未必能再充当外国投资
者的损失缓冲垫。
如果纳斯达克、美元指数（ DXY ）与美国实际利率三者同步下⾏，市场正在定价的将不仅是仓
位的技术性调整，⽽是对整个 AI 繁荣周期的系统性重估。这意味着下⼀轮 AI 抛售，有可能演变为
⼀场美元危机。
20 万亿美元的脆弱敞⼝
据德意志银⾏估算，外国投资者持有的美国股票资产中，约 90% 的价值来⾃累积的价格升值，

⽽⾮实际净购⼊。换⾔之，这⼀庞⼤头⼨⾼度依赖美国股市估值的持续维持。
更关键的是，外国股票资⾦流⼊⽬前已承担了美国经常账⼾⾚字的全部融资需求，美元因此与
股票市场周期深度绑定。⼀旦股市出现⼤幅调整，美元的资⾦来源将直接承压。
对冲机制在美国本⼟冲击下失效
过去⼗年，每当美国股市下跌，美元⾛强往往帮助外国投资者对冲了部分损失。但这⼀保护机
制有⼀个前提：冲击来⾃外部。若⻛险源头在美国本⾝，股市与美元可能同向下跌，对冲逻辑
随之⽡解。
StoneX 的数据显⽰， AI 情绪与美元之间的五⽇滚动相关系数⾼达 +0.97 ，⽽ 20 ⽇相关系数仅为
+0.31 。这意味着短期内两者已形成⾼度同步 ——AI 信⼼⼀旦动摇，美元将同步承压。
对于未对冲的外国投资者⽽⾔，股票和汇率的双重亏损将造成双重打击。即便他们不抛售股
票，单纯提⾼货币对冲⽐例的操作本⾝，也会形成额外的抛售美元压⼒，加剧汇率下⾏。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

实际利率是关键验证信号
德意志银⾏认为， AI 资本开⽀预期的急速膨胀，是推动美国股市与实际利率同步⾛⾼的重要驱动
⼒之⼀。因此，实际利率的⾛向将成为判断市场性质的关键信号。
若科技股、美元与美国实际利率三者同步下⾏，将表明投资者开始折现⼀个更疲弱的 AI 投资周
期 —— 这将把⼀次技术性股市修正，升级为更⼴泛的宏观重新定价。相反，若实际利率维持⾼
位，则调整更可能只是仓位层⾯的波动。
做空美元或成 AI 修正的隐性交易
⾯对 AI 潜在回调，最直观的交易是做空科技股。但德意志银⾏指出，不那么显眼、却可能更具威
⼒的交易是做空美元。
⼀旦以美国为中⼼的股票冲击成真，外国投资者将被迫重新审视其在这 20 万亿美元资产上的货
币对冲策略，传统的股票 - 美元正相关关系可能发⽣逆转。投资者需要同时盯紧纳斯达克、 DXY
与美国实际利率三个指标。若三者共同⾛低，市场所定价的⻛险将远⼤于⼀次普通的仓位出清
—— 下⼀轮 AI 抛售，或将以⼀场美元抛售收场。

### Page 3

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

Document 311: Global Strategy Views_ Competition for Capital

Source PDF: all report pdf/Global Strategy Views_ Competition for Capital.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs Publishing
Date: 2026-09-17
Pages: 12
Status: ok

### Page 1

1. There are two themes dominating our investor conversations: the impact of AI and 
the rise in interest rates. The two issues are linked as demand for capital from both 
the private and public sectors increase. In the private sector a surge in capex 
spending to fund AI infrastructure has eaten into free cash flow and forced 
companies to raise more in debt and equity markets. Meanwhile, government 
borrowing needs have increased as priorities shift towards upgrading critical 
infrastructure, energy security and defense at a time when cyclical inflationary 
pressures driven by higher energy prices are also resulting in higher policy rates. The 
combination has pushed up the cost of capital. As recently as 2022, for example, 
30-year bond yields in Germany and Japan were close to zero (Exhibit 1). A rise in 
yields, together with more uncertainty (over geopolitics and the future impact of AI) 
have, in combination, pushed up the cost of capital.

2. This rise in yields comes after a near-record period of equity outperformance 
relative to bonds over 10-year holding periods (Exhibit 2).

Exhibit 1: Rising yields and uncertainty have increased the cost of capital 
30-year government bond benchmark yields (%) 
          













### Page 2

As equities have continued to outperform bonds, equity risk premia have fallen back to 
levels last seen in the late 1990s, leaving equity markets more vulnerable to further 
increases in bond yields. That said, in the US and Japan, the ERP has bounced off recent 
lows (Exhibit 3).

3. The impact of rising bond yields, however, depends not only on the level of yields, but 
also on the reasons as well as the speed of the adjustment (Exhibit 4). It is the speed 
issue that has caused the biggest impact in the recent past. As our US strategists note, 
stocks have usually generated positive returns alongside rising interest rates unless the 
pace of rising rates exceeded 2+ standard deviations and this is a pattern that is broadly

been flat (Japan and Europe) or have fallen (the US, Asia and EM).

5. While US equities have driven the bulk of equity outperformance for much of the 
fifteen years post the financial crisis, the improvement in earnings across regions, and 
the sharper de-rating in the US, has resulted in equity performance being 
broader-based by geography. Since 2025, the US equity market has been the weakest of 
the major regions (Exhibit 6), a turnaround from the dominant trend since the financial 
crisis and increasing investor returns from a more geographically diversified portfolio.

6. Supportive profit growth has been accompanied by positive earnings revisions, with 
2026 and 2027 estimates moving higher across major regions. There are four broad 
areas which have been driving much of this profit growth. First, technology earnings 
continue to be very robust. Second, rising energy prices have pushed up profits in the 
commodity sector. Third, banks have generally enjoyed strong earnings backed by 
positive nominal GDP growth, steep yield curves and strong private sector balance 
sheets. Finally, sectors such as Industrials have benefited from the surge in AI capex 
spending which has spilled over into improved revenues for the ‘pick and shovels’ of AI 
infrastructure. The breadth of the earnings’ growth has also increased the opportunity 
for investors to diversify across sectors as well as countries.

7. While technology profit growth has remained strong, the surge in capex spending 
among the hyperscalers has increasingly eaten through their free cash flow (Exhibit 8) 
prompting companies to look for alternative sources of funding, in the credit and equity 
markets.  The capex spending growth for AA-rated issuers has been substantial: the 65% 
year-over-year growth in Q2 marks the 10th consecutive quarter that aggregate AA 
capex growth exceeds 35%. They have also turned to the convertible bond market 
where volume has also increased year to date reaching $135 billion in the US, with 
AI-related borrowers driving 44% of total issuance, on our estimates. 
Our Credit team raised their full-year US IG gross issuance forecast by $200bn to $2.3 
trillion and AI-related issuers account for a quarter of USD IG gross supply this year, 
pushing overall supply to a year-to-date record.

has reduced the value of their future cash flows and triggered a de-rating in the biggest 
companies.  The dominant capex hyperscalers in the US now have a forward PE close to 
the average of the rest of the market.

8. Software companies have also de-rated on the back of fears of disruption from AI 
while memory and chips stocks have also experienced lower multiples as their earnings

surge. Globally technology has seen its PE ratio moderate, falling below its 20-year 
median (Exhibit 10). Meanwhile, the spending of these companies has spilled over into 
better earnings and more visible cash flows in many other parts of the economy. 
Healthcare, Consumer Discretionary and Industrials are all more expensive in absolute 
terms than IT, and Industrials has re-rated and now has a PE close to the 90th percentile 
of its 20-year range.

9. For these reasons, we have argued that Technology equities have not experienced a 
classic bubble because valuation levels are much lower than in previous financial 
bubbles. Nevertheless, the risk for investors is less about valuation and more whether 
there is an unsustainable earnings bubble. Bubbles in earnings, rather than valuations, 
have occurred in previous periods.  The Bank sector, for example, briefly became the 
biggest sector in the S&P 500 in the run up to the financial crisis of 2008/09 (Exhibit 11). 
Unlike the technology bubble of the late 1990s, or the Japanese bubble of the late 
1980s, Bank stocks did not experience a major valuation bubble at the time, but the 
surge in earnings that drove the sector’s outperformance proved to be unsustainable.

### Page 8

There are, however, important differences to the Tech sector today. The earnings of the 
banks were boosted by sharply rising leverage and the asset that they were financing – 
US real estate – did experience a valuation bubble. When the housing market collapsed, 
pushing economies into recession, it triggered a sharp fall in underlying earnings for the 
banking sector. There are important differences today. First, technology profits remain 
very robust, and balance sheets are strong overall. Second, interest coverage ratios for 
the aggregate S&P 500 and its median stock, for example, rank in the 99th and 68th 
percentiles relative to the past 20 years. Third, demand for compute is accelerating and 
outstripping supply; Microsoft recently declared their intent to triple data centre 
capacity in 6 years. The bottlenecks are increasingly power, land and data centre 
capacity rather than a lack of compute demand. At our annual Communacopia 
Technology Conference, Nvidia reiterated its 2030 AI TAM outlook to $3tn-$4tn and 
supply constraints continue to be broad based. Nonetheless, any slowdown in profit 
growth, in an environment of a much higher cost of capital, could put downward 
pressure on equity prices, reducing confidence in future cash flows across the ecosystem 
from the hyperscalers to the ‘pick and shovels’ that have been benefiting from the capex 
boom.  
10. Given the near-term risks in bond markets, we remain neutral on equities, alongside 
other asset classes on a 3-month horizon, but continue to be overweight equities over 
12 months. While profit growth is likely to slow from the recent strong pace, ongoing 
growth in nominal GDP should underpin equity markets and the earnings cycle 
propelling equity returns even without any valuation expansion from current levels and 
our economists continue to see a low risk of recession. As our asset allocation team has 
shown, earnings growth and ROE are likely to continue to be the key drivers of equity 
performance relative to bonds, and we expect these fundamentals to remain supportive 
for equity returns. Nevertheless, we continue to recommend well diversified equity 
exposure as opportunities continue to broaden across geographies, sectors and factors.

Stock correlations are at multi-year lows across the major equity markets (Exhibit 12).

Even within sectors like technology, correlations have fallen sharply (Exhibit 13). The 
‘magnificent seven’ that was long seen as a homogeneous group of technology winners 
has also seen significant dispersion of returns. Differentiation, diversification and alpha 
generation are likely to remain the key drivers of equity returns. With higher bond yields, 
valuations are not likely to rise making earnings the critical driver of returns for investors.

Nvidia 與  Tesla 聯⼿注資三星電機，除了試圖打破  ABF 載板⿓頭⻑
期短缺及漲價壟斷，是否也為了採⽤三星 2nm 晶圓代⼯鋪路 ?
ANDREW LU ON GLOBAL SEMI/TECHS

OCT 23 ∙ PAID
READ IN APP
What’s new?
2026 年  10 ⽉  22 ⽇⾄  23 ⽇，路透社（ Reuters ）、南韓《每⽇經濟新聞》
（ Maeil Business Newspaper ）與多家科技產業媒體同步披露：全球  AI 晶
⽚霸主輝達（ NVIDIA ）與電動⾞及具⾝智能巨頭特斯拉（ Tesla ），聯⼿向南
韓三星電機（ Samsung Electro-Mechanics ）注資約  28 億美元（折合約
3.7 兆韓元），⽤於其在南韓世宗市（ Sejong ）⼯廠⼤規模新建專⽤⾼階覆晶
球柵陣列（ FC-BGA / ABF Substract ）半導體封裝載板產線。此前幾天（ 10
⽉  19 ⽇），三星電機已於官⽅聲明中正式確認與全球⼤型企業達成戰略夥伴協
議，將⾃  2027 年 1 ⽉ 1 ⽇起正式向⼤客戶供應⾼階半導體封裝載板（三星电机
与全球⼤型企业签订 “ 存储器半导体⽤封装基板 ” ⻓期供应合同（ LTA ））。報導

指出，在  28 億美元注資總承諾中，輝達預計出資約  2.6 兆韓元，特斯拉出資
約  1.1 兆韓元。這筆巨額資⾦旨在為輝達下⼀代  Blackwell Ultra GB300
NVL72 超級計算叢集、 Rubin 架構  GPU ，以及特斯拉即將量產的  HW5 ⾃動
駕駛⾞載晶⽚與  Optimus 機器⼈神經網絡晶⽚，鎖定最為緊缺的⾼層數、⼤
⾯積先進封裝載板產能。
付費訂閱戶可分享受惠，受害公司 / 產業，⾮共識想
法，及建議
Subscribed

### Page 2

Positively aﬀect what supply chain vendors and
explains why?
三星電機  (Samsung Electro-Mechanics, 009150.KS) ：獲得輝達與
特斯拉兩⼤全球最⼤  AI 算⼒買家的巨額資本與⻑約鎖定，使其⼀舉打破傳統
⽇系（ Ibiden, Shinko) 與台系廠商 ( 欣興，南電）的⻑期壟斷，躍升為全球
AI 載板⼀線核⼼主⼒。
ABF 味之素積層膜材料商（⽇本味之素  Ajinomoto ）與載板設備商：⼤⾯
積  FC-BGA 載板層數已⾼達  20 層以上，良率爬坡極耗材料，直接帶動上游特
種介電樹脂與雷射鑽孔設備採購放量。
Negatively aﬀect what supply chain vendors and
explains why?
傳統載板雙寡頭（⽇本揖斐電  Ibiden 、新光電氣  Shinko ）及台系傳統載板
廠（欣興、南電）：輝達與特斯拉主動扶持三星電機建⽴新載板產能，短期可

能看不出來，但⻑期將削弱現有⽇台供應商過去兩年在⾼階載板定價上的絕對
話語權與超額利潤率，並改變市場份額。以 28 億美元投資來看，雖然僅佔 4%
的三星電機市值，但確明顯超過  Ibiden 及欣興近 10-15 億美元的年度資本開
⽀。
晶圓代⼯⿓頭台積電及封測產業鏈：從 Nvidia 及 Tesla 在 HBM DRAM ， DDR
DRAM ， NAND 與三星電⼦的採購關係，擴⼤到三星電機  ABF 載板產能擴充
的注資，我們不排除  Nvidia 及 Tesla 將進⼀步採⽤三星電⼦ 2nm （近期良率
有明顯改善）晶圓代⼯製程及封測技術，讓三星集團進⾏  AI 訓練及推理晶⽚
製造整合服務來搶台廠份額。
未獲得載板保證的⼆線  ASIC 晶⽚設計公司：頂級載板產能被巨頭包攬，使資
⾦鏈較弱的中⼩  AI 晶⽚公司⾯臨載板交期延⻑⾄  50 週以上的斷鏈危機。
Our outside the box non-consensus thinking
特斯拉參投載板是「實體  AI 製造去台化 / 去⽇化」的戰略預演：市場多將注意

⼒集中於輝達，但特斯拉⾸次拿出  1.1 兆韓元直投載板廠更具震撼性。這證明
⾺斯克已深刻意識到 ——HW5 晶⽚與百萬台  Optimus 的真正死⽳不僅僅是
晶圓代⼯產能短缺，⽽也包括 ABF 封裝載板⾯積及厚度激增引發的良率瓶頸。
特斯拉直接以⾞企⾝分下場穿透供應鏈投資底層零組件，正在重構半導體採購
範式。
「晶⽚⾯積膨脹」倒逼載板成為半導體新護城河：隨著晶⽚尺⼨光罩極限朝
10x 以上突破（ Reticle Limit ），單顆  GB300 或  HW5 載板尺⼨已超過
100mm x 100mm ，相當於⼀塊⼩型主機板。載板製造已從傳統  PCB 加⼯
轉變為「類晶圓級微影⼯程」，載板產能的鎖定甚⾄⽐搶奪台積電  CoWoS 配
額更具決定性。

### Page 3

Our advises to investors
我們建議投資⼈關注三星電機（ Samsung Electro-Mechanics ）及其核⼼材
料設備供應商在產業份額的變化，尤其是在輝達與特斯拉雙重認證下，其⾼階
載板營收佔⽐將在未來兩年由不⾜  15% 是否能飆升⾄  30% 以上，迎來估值
重估。適度獲利了結估值處於歷史⾼點的傳統純載板標的，防範  2027 年三星
電機新產能開出後，⾏業整體⽑利率⾯臨均值回歸的競爭壓⼒。三星電機產線
在⼤⾯積  FC-BGA 的翹曲控制（ Warpage Control ）與熱膨脹良率爬坡仍值
得觀察。

首次 評等  
買進 (Buy) 
財報、財測 大幅優於預期， SI(AI) 成為最大業務  
⚫ 財報、財測皆顯著優於市場共識 
⚫ SI 部門成為第一大業務，且轉虧為盈  
⚫ Starlink 用 戶數穩定成長  
財報、財測顯著優於市場共識，並實現虧轉盈：SpaceX 於 10/20 舉行財
報會議， FY3Q26 營收 146 億美元 (QoQ+87% ，YoY+ 161 %)，優於市
場共識 129 億美元， GAAP 毛利率 62.4% ，EPS 0.35 美元，顯著優於
市場共識 0.15 美元，且由虧轉盈。公司本次維持 2026 年底年化經常
性收入 (ARR) 目標 1,000 億美元不變，並給予 1Q27 營收目標為
330~370 億美元，大幅優於市場共識 244 億美元，公司優異財報及財
測表現使盤後股價上漲 8%。 
SI(AI) 部門成為第一大業務，且轉虧為盈： SI 部門 FY3Q26 營收 76
億美元，首次成為公司最大業務，主要受惠雲端服務合約及 Grok Bot
帶動。其中 SI 解決方案與基礎建設業務營收 70 億美元，廣告營收
5.4 億美元 (YoY+19%) 。部門調整後 EBITDA 為 50 億美元，營業利
潤 4.31 億美元，由 FY2Q26 營業虧損 13 億美元轉虧為盈。公司目前
算力規模已達 2.2 GW ，預期年底將達 2.5~3GW ，目前每月上線約 20
萬台 GB300 ，2027 年目標則為 13 GW ，將以 Vera Rubin 為主，預期
FY2Q27 末將上線 44 萬台 VR 伺服器。  
Starlink 用 戶數每年高速成長： Connectivity 部門 FY3Q26 營收 52
億美元 (YoY+69% ，QoQ+22%) ，毛利率 55% ，FY3Q26 消費用戶數
達 1,360 萬，淨增加 160 萬用戶。 ARPU(Average Revenue Per User)
方面， FY3Q26 ARPU 69 美元 /月(QoQ+5%) ，低於 FY3Q25 的 83 美
元/月，主因為公司服務範圍由原先歐美轉向南美、非洲、東南亞等
地區，因此在定價策略上進行調整。 Starlink Mobile 手機直連業務月
活躍 用戶突破 2,200 萬(QoQ+37%) ，已獲 FCC 批准發射 15,000 顆
Mobile V2 衛星並收購低頻段頻譜，預計 2028 年初開通服務。  
評價： 我們認為 SpaceX 於太空產業位處龍頭地位，且目前並未有競
爭對手，加上 Grok 帶來強勁成長動能 ，因此 首次 評等 給予「買進」，
評價面考量公司位於高速成長初期，加上公司業務多為訂閱模式，
以 PS 方式給予評價 ，目標價 220 美元(2027 每股營收 10.8 美元 × 20
倍 PSR)。風險因子 :低軌衛星用戶數不如預期、 AI 資料中心建置需
求轉弱 。

SpaceX 為全球 太空產業龍頭 ：SpaceX 業務主要分為 Connectivity 、AI
及 Space 三大部門。 Connectivity 以 Starlink 衛星連網服務為核心，涵
蓋消費者、企業及政府客戶業務，包括 Starshield 相關服務； SI(Super 
Intelligence) 部門以 SpaceXSI( 前 xAI) 為核心，涵蓋 AI 運算服務、 Grok
及 X(前 Twitter) 平台 ；Space 部門則涵蓋 火箭 發射服務與太空計畫開發
業務。公司 FY3Q26 業務比重分別為 Connectivity 36%、SI 52%、Space 
12% 。 Space X 在衛星通訊與火箭發射領域均具領先地位 ， 2025 年
SpaceX 共發射 165 次火箭， 全 球 火 箭 發 射共 325 次， 其 市 占 率 約
50.8% ，而在衛星通訊領域， Starlink 則為目前唯一可提供寬頻衛星通
訊服務給大規模用戶的供應商。  
圖表一：衛星產業市場規模

資料來源： SIA ，中信投顧整理  
Falcon 9 開啟火箭技術革命 ： 傳統 火箭多為一次性 設計 ，主要結構於
發 射後 拋棄， 使每 次發射 任務 均須重 新製 造與整 備， 導致 火 箭發 射成
本高昂。而 SpaceX 的獵鷹 9 號(Falcon 9 )於 2015 年首次完成一級火箭
回收，並於 2017 年實現回收助推器再次飛行，突破火箭一次性使用限
制，降低火箭發射成本， 使衛星寬頻通訊商業化可行性提高。 Falcon 9
每次發射衛星 報價 約在 3,200 美元 /公斤 ，SpaceX 自用成本更低 ，而最
新 的傳 統火 箭(Ariane 6) 發射 報價 則 約在 6,000 美元 /公斤 ， 相比 之 下
SpaceX 在火箭發射成本上有顯著優勢。 而 下 一 代 火 箭星艦 (Starship)
推測每公斤成本約在 600 美元 /公斤， 且目標 為全部回收並 將發射成本
進一步降低至 200 美元 /公斤 ，有望進一步帶動衛星發射數量 。

Document 378: The Institute Employment Report_ September 2026 Bank of America Institute analysis shows slower payroll growth, declining unemployment payments and continued strength in lower-inco

Source PDF: all report pdf/The Institute Employment Report_ September 2026 Bank of America Institute analysis shows slower payroll growth, declining unemployment payments and continued strength in lower-inco_223e3a6a.pdf

Economy 
The Institute Employment Report: September 2026 
07 October 2026

Key takeaways 
• Job growth softened further in September, according to Bank of America's deposit data, with growth of 1.4% year-over-year 
(YoY), from 1.5% in August. But falling unemployment payments in September indicate a relatively healthy overall picture. 
• Lower-income households' wage growth remains strongest, despite a narrowing gap. After-tax wage growth eased to 4.5% YoY 
for lower-income households in September, while rising to 3.8% and 3.7% for middle- and higher-income households, 
respectively. 
• Lower tax withholdings and a relative strengthening in the younger/lower-income end of the labor market are driving this story. 
Additionally, average hours have risen in some lower-paid industries, which is also boosting pay packets.

Job growth slowed in September, but unemployment trends are benign  
Our estimate of job growth – which uses Bank of America deposit data to track changes in the number of customer accounts 
receiving a paycheck (see Methodology) – slowed further in September, to 1.4% year-over-year (YoY) from 1.5% YoY in August. 
The data can be noisy, partly due to seasonal variation and differences in pay-period timing, but in our view, the latest reading is 
consistent with the easing in payrolls growth seen in the September Bureau of Labor Statistics (BLS) data (Exhibit 1). 
Still, despite slower hiring, there is little sign of a rise in joblessness in Bank of America data. Unemployment payments into 
customer accounts showed a YoY decline in September (Exhibit 2). So, overall, labor market trends appear resilient.

After-tax wage growth cooled for lower-income, but rose for other cohorts 
Lower-income wage growth eased to 4.5% YoY in September from 4.7% YoY in August, while wage growth for middle- and 
higher-income households rose to 3.8% and 3.7% YoY, respectively. Overall, these changes were modest, and lower-income 
households are continuing to see faster after-tax wage growth than other cohorts, a notable turnaround from the “K-shaped” 
pattern we observed over much of 2025 and 2026 (Exhibit 3). 
Exhibit 3: Lower-income wage growth remains strongest, despite a narrowing gap 
After-tax wage and salary growth by household income terciles, based on Bank of America aggregated consumer deposit account data (three-month moving 
average, YoY%, SA)

Source: Bank of America internal data 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
In our view, the pick-up in lower- and middle-income households’ after-tax wage growth continues to reflect the impact of the 
One Big Beautiful Bill Act (OBBA) in reducing tax withholdings, alongside an underlying rise in job-to-job changes at the 
younger/lower-income end of the labor market (for more, read: The Institute Employment Report: August 2026). Consistent with 
that underlying pick up, BLS data shows a solid YoY rise in average hours worked in several relatively lower-paid industries, such 
as manufacturing, retail and other services. Hourly workers in these industries are likely seeing rising paychecks as a result 
(Exhibit 4).
